```
1	# Copyright (c) 2023-2025, Songlin Yang, Yu Zhang
2	
3	
4	import torch
5	import triton
6	import triton.language as tl
7	
8	from fla.ops.utils import prepare_chunk_offsets
9	from fla.ops.utils.op import exp
10	from fla.utils import autotune_cache_kwargs, check_shared_mem
11	
12	BKV_LIST = [32, 64] if check_shared_mem() else [16, 32]
13	
14	
15	@triton.heuristics({
16	    'USE_INITIAL_STATE': lambda args: args['h0'] is not None,
17	    'STORE_FINAL_STATE': lambda args: args['ht'] is not None,
18	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
19	})
20	@triton.autotune(
21	    configs=[
22	        triton.Config({'BK': BK, 'BV': BV}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
23	        for BK in BKV_LIST
24	        for BV in BKV_LIST
25	        for num_warps in [1, 2, 4, 8]
26	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
27	    ],
28	    key=['BT', 'USE_G', 'USE_GK', 'USE_GV'],
29	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
30	)
31	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
32	def chunk_fwd_kernel_h(
33	    k,
34	    v,
35	    h,
36	    g,
37	    g_gamma,
38	    gk,
39	    gv,
40	    h0,
41	    ht,
42	    cu_seqlens,
43	    split_offsets,
44	    T,
45	    H: tl.constexpr,
46	    K: tl.constexpr,
47	    V: tl.constexpr,
48	    BT: tl.constexpr,
49	    BS: tl.constexpr,
50	    BK: tl.constexpr,
51	    BV: tl.constexpr,
52	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
53	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
54	    USE_GK: tl.constexpr,
55	    USE_GV: tl.constexpr,
56	    USE_INITIAL_STATE: tl.constexpr,
57	    STORE_FINAL_STATE: tl.constexpr,
58	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
59	):
60	    i_k, i_v, i_nh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
61	    i_n, i_h = i_nh // H, i_nh % H
62	    if IS_VARLEN:
63	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
64	        T = eos - bos
65	        NT, NS = tl.cdiv(T, BT), tl.cdiv(T, BS)
66	        boh = tl.load(split_offsets + i_n).to(tl.int32)
67	    else:
68	        bos, eos = i_n * T, i_n * T + T
69	        NT, NS = tl.cdiv(T, BT), tl.cdiv(T, BS)
70	        boh = i_n * NS
71	    NTS = BS // BT
72	
73	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
74	        # decay rate given the head index
75	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
76	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
77	
78	    # [BK, BV]
79	    b_h = tl.zeros([BK, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
80	    if USE_INITIAL_STATE:
81	        p_h0 = tl.make_block_ptr(h0 + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
82	        b_h = tl.load(p_h0, boundary_check=(0, 1)).to(tl.float32)
83	
84	    for i_t in range(NT):
85	        i_s = i_t // NTS
86	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
87	        p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
88	
89	        o_h = ((boh + i_s) * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
90	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h + o_h, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
91	
92	        if i_t % NTS == 0:
93	            tl.store(p_h, b_h.to(p_h.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
94	        # [BK, BT]
95	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
96	        # [BT, BV]
97	        b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
98	        last_idx = min((i_t + 1) * BT, T) - 1
99	
100	        # scalar decay
101	        if USE_G:
102	            b_g_last = tl.load(g + bos * H + last_idx * H + i_h)
103	            p_g = g + bos*H + (i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)) * H + i_h
104	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, mask=(i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT) < T), other=0.)
105	            b_h *= exp(b_g_last)
106	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_g_last - b_g)[:, None]).to(b_v.dtype)
107	
108	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
109	            b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
110	            b_h *= exp(b_g_last)
111	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_g_last - b_g)[:, None]).to(b_v.dtype)
112	
113	        # vector decay, h = Diag(gk) @ h
114	        if USE_GK:
115	            p_gk = tl.make_block_ptr(gk + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
116	            p_gk_last = gk + (bos + last_idx) * H*K + i_h * K + i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK)
117	
118	            b_gk_last = tl.load(p_gk_last, mask=(i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK) < K), other=0.)
119	            b_h *= exp(b_gk_last)[:, None]
120	
121	            b_gk = tl.load(p_gk, boundary_check=(0, 1))
122	            b_k = (b_k * exp(b_gk_last[:, None] - b_gk)).to(b_k.dtype)
123	
124	        # vector decay, h = h @ Diag(gv)
125	        if USE_GV:
126	            p_gv = tl.make_block_ptr(gv + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
127	            p_gv_last = gv + (bos + last_idx) * H*V + i_h * V + i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV)
128	
129	            b_gv_last = tl.load(p_gv_last, mask=(i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV) < V), other=0.)
130	            b_h *= exp(b_gv_last)[None, :]
131	
132	            b_gv = tl.load(p_gv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
133	            b_v = (b_v * exp(b_gv_last[None, :] - b_gv)).to(b_v.dtype)
134	
135	        b_h += tl.dot(b_k, b_v)
136	
137	    if STORE_FINAL_STATE:
138	        p_ht = tl.make_block_ptr(ht + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
139	        tl.store(p_ht, b_h.to(p_ht.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
140	
141	
142	@triton.heuristics({
143	    'STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT': lambda args: args['dh0'] is not None,
144	    'USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT': lambda args: args['dht'] is not None,
145	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
146	})
147	@triton.autotune(
148	    configs=[
149	        triton.Config({'BK': BK, 'BV': BV}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
150	        for BK in BKV_LIST
151	        for BV in BKV_LIST
152	        for num_warps in [1, 2, 4, 8]
153	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
154	    ],
155	    key=['BT', 'USE_G', 'USE_GK', 'USE_GV'],
156	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
157	)
158	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
159	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dh(
160	    q,
161	    g,
162	    g_gamma,
163	    gk,
164	    gv,
165	    do,
166	    dh,
167	    dht,
168	    dh0,
169	    cu_seqlens,
170	    split_offsets,
171	    scale,
172	    T,
173	    HQ: tl.constexpr,
174	    H: tl.constexpr,
175	    K: tl.constexpr,
176	    V: tl.constexpr,
177	    BT: tl.constexpr,
178	    BS: tl.constexpr,
179	    BK: tl.constexpr,
180	    BV: tl.constexpr,
181	    NG: tl.constexpr,
182	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
183	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
184	    USE_GK: tl.constexpr,
185	    USE_GV: tl.constexpr,
186	    STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT: tl.constexpr,
187	    USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT: tl.constexpr,
188	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
189	):
190	    i_k, i_v, i_nh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
191	    i_n, i_hq = i_nh // HQ, i_nh % HQ
192	    i_h = i_hq // NG
193	    if IS_VARLEN:
194	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
195	        T = eos - bos
196	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
197	        NS = tl.cdiv(T, BS)
198	        boh = tl.load(split_offsets + i_n).to(tl.int32)
199	    else:
200	        bos, eos = i_n * T, i_n * T + T
201	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
202	        NS = tl.cdiv(T, BS)
203	        boh = i_n * NS
204	
205	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
206	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
207	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
208	
209	    # [BK, BV]
210	    b_dh = tl.zeros([BK, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
211	    if USE_FINAL_STATE_GRADIENT:
212	        p_dht = tl.make_block_ptr(dht + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
213	        b_dh += tl.load(p_dht, boundary_check=(0, 1)).to(tl.float32)
214	
215	    for i_t in range(NT - 1, -1, -1):
216	        i_s = i_t // (BS // BT)
217	        o_dh = ((boh + i_s) * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
218	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh + o_dh, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
219	
220	        if i_t % (BS // BT) == 0:
221	            tl.store(p_dh, b_dh.to(p_dh.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
222	        last_idx = min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1
223	        # [BK, BT]
224	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q + (bos*HQ + i_hq) * K, (K, T), (1, HQ*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
225	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do + (bos*HQ + i_hq) * V, (T, V), (HQ*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
226	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
227	        b_q = (b_q * scale).to(b_q.dtype)
228	        # [BT, BV]
229	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
230	
231	        if USE_G:
232	            p_g = g + (bos + i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)) * H + i_h
233	            b_g_last = tl.load(g + (bos + last_idx) * H + i_h)
234	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, mask=(i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT) < T), other=0.)
235	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_g)[None, :]).to(b_q.dtype)
236	            b_dh *= exp(b_g_last)
237	
238	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
239	            b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
240	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_g)[None, :]).to(b_q.dtype)
241	            b_dh *= exp(b_g_last)
242	
243	        if USE_GK:
244	            p_gk = tl.make_block_ptr(gk + (bos*H + i_h) * K, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
245	            p_gk_last = gk + (bos + last_idx) * H*K + i_h * K + i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK)
246	
247	            b_gk = tl.load(p_gk, boundary_check=(0, 1))
248	            b_q = (b_q * exp(b_gk)).to(b_q.dtype)
249	            b_gk_last = tl.load(p_gk_last, mask=(i_k * BK + tl.arange(0, BK) < K), other=0.)
250	            b_dh *= exp(b_gk_last)[:, None]
251	
252	        if USE_GV:
253	            p_gv = tl.make_block_ptr(gv + (bos*H + i_h) * V, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
254	            p_gv_last = gv + (bos + last_idx) * H*V + i_h * V + i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV)
255	
256	            b_gv = tl.load(p_gv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
257	            b_do = (b_do * exp(b_gv))
258	
259	            b_gv_last = tl.load(p_gv_last, mask=(i_v * BV + tl.arange(0, BV) < V), other=0.)
260	            b_dh *= exp(b_gv_last)[None, :]
261	
262	        b_dh += tl.dot(b_q, b_do.to(b_q.dtype))
263	
264	    if STORE_INITIAL_STATE_GRADIENT:
265	        p_dh0 = tl.make_block_ptr(dh0 + i_nh * K*V, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
266	        tl.store(p_dh0, b_dh.to(p_dh0.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
267	
268	
269	def chunk_fwd_h(
270	    k: torch.Tensor,
271	    v: torch.Tensor,
272	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
273	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
274	    gk: torch.Tensor | None = None,
275	    gv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
276	    h0: torch.Tensor | None = None,
277	    output_final_state: bool = False,
278	    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor | None = None,
279	    chunk_size: int = 64,
280	    split_size: int | None = None,
281	    states_in_fp32: bool = False,
282	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
283	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
284	    BT = chunk_size
285	    BS = BT if split_size is None else split_size
286	    assert BS % BT == 0, f"The `split_size` (got {BS}) must be a multiple of `chunk_size` {BT}"
287	    # N: the actual number of sequences in the batch with either equal or variable lengths
288	    if cu_seqlens is None:
289	        N, NS, split_offsets = B, triton.cdiv(T, BS), None
290	    else:
291	        split_offsets = prepare_chunk_offsets(cu_seqlens, BS)
292	        N, NS = len(cu_seqlens) - 1, split_offsets[-1].item()
293	
294	    h = k.new_empty(B, NS, H, K, V, dtype=k.dtype if not states_in_fp32 else torch.float)
295	    ht = k.new_empty(N, H, K, V, dtype=torch.float) if output_final_state else None
296	    def grid(meta): return (triton.cdiv(K, meta['BK']), triton.cdiv(V, meta['BV']), N * H)
297	    chunk_fwd_kernel_h[grid](
298	        k=k,
299	        v=v,
300	        h=h,
301	        g=g,
302	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
303	        gk=gk,
304	        gv=gv,
305	        h0=h0,
306	        ht=ht,
307	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
308	        split_offsets=split_offsets,
309	        T=T,
310	        H=H,
311	        K=K,
312	        V=V,
313	        BT=BT,
314	        BS=BS,
315	        USE_G=g is not None,
316	        USE_G_GAMMA=g_gamma is not None,
317	        USE_GK=gk is not None,
318	        USE_GV=gv is not None,
319	    )
320	    return h, ht
321	
322	
323	def chunk_bwd_dh(
324	    q: torch.Tensor,
325	    k: torch.Tensor,
326	    v: torch.Tensor,
327	    do: torch.Tensor,
328	    h0: torch.Tensor,
329	    dht: torch.Tensor,
330	    scale: float,
331	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
332	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
333	    gk: torch.Tensor | None = None,
334	    gv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
335	    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor | None = None,
336	    chunk_size: int = 64,
337	    split_size: int | None = None,
338	    states_in_fp32: bool = False,
339	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
340	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
341	    HQ = q.shape[2]
342	    BT = chunk_size
343	    BS = BT if split_size is None else split_size
344	    assert BS % BT == 0, f"The `split_size` (got {BS}) must be a multiple of `chunk_size` {BT}"
345	    # N: the actual number of sequences in the batch with either equal or variable lengths
346	    # NG: number of groups in GQA
347	    if cu_seqlens is None:
348	        N, NS, split_offsets = B, triton.cdiv(T, BS), None
349	    else:
350	        split_offsets = prepare_chunk_offsets(cu_seqlens, BS)
351	        N, NS = len(cu_seqlens) - 1, split_offsets[-1].item()
352	    NG = HQ // H
353	
354	    dh = k.new_empty(B, NS, HQ, K, V, dtype=k.dtype if not states_in_fp32 else torch.float)
355	    dh0 = torch.empty_like(h0, dtype=torch.float) if h0 is not None else None
356	
357	    def grid(meta): return (triton.cdiv(K, meta['BK']), triton.cdiv(V, meta['BV']), N * H)
358	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dh[grid](
359	        q=q,
360	        g=g,
361	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
362	        gk=gk,
363	        gv=gv,
364	        do=do,
365	        dh=dh,
366	        dht=dht,
367	        dh0=dh0,
368	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
369	        split_offsets=split_offsets,
370	        scale=scale,
371	        T=T,
372	        HQ=HQ,
373	        H=H,
374	        K=K,
375	        V=V,
376	        BT=BT,
377	        BS=BS,
378	        NG=NG,
379	        USE_G=g is not None,
380	        USE_G_GAMMA=g_gamma is not None,
381	        USE_GK=gk is not None,
382	        USE_GV=gv is not None,
383	    )
384	    return dh, dh0
385
```

```
3	import torch

7	from fla.ops.utils import prepare_chunk_indices
8	from fla.ops.utils.op import exp
9	from fla.utils import IS_NVIDIA_HOPPER, autotune_cache_kwargs, check_shared_mem
10	
11	BKV_LIST = [64, 128] if check_shared_mem() else [32, 64]
12	NUM_WARPS = [2, 4] if IS_NVIDIA_HOPPER else [2, 4, 8]
13	
14	
15	@triton.heuristics({
16	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
17	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
18	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
19	})
20	@triton.autotune(
21	    configs=[
22	        triton.Config({'BK': 128, 'BV': 128}, num_warps=8, num_stages=3),
23	        triton.Config({'BK': 64, 'BV': 64}, num_warps=4, num_stages=3),
24	        triton.Config({'BK': 32, 'BV': 32}, num_warps=2, num_stages=3),
25	    ],
26	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT'],
27	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
28	)
29	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
30	def chunk_fwd_kernel_o(
31	    q,
32	    k,
33	    v,
34	    h,
35	    g,
36	    g_gamma,
37	    o,
38	    cu_seqlens,
39	    chunk_indices,
40	    scale,
41	    T,
42	    H: tl.constexpr,
43	    K: tl.constexpr,
44	    V: tl.constexpr,
45	    BT: tl.constexpr,
46	    BK: tl.constexpr,
47	    BV: tl.constexpr,
48	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
49	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
50	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
51	):
52	    i_v, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
53	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
54	
55	    if IS_VARLEN:
56	        i_tg = i_t
57	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
58	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
59	        T = eos - bos
60	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
61	    else:
62	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
63	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
64	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
65	
66	    # offset calculation
67	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
68	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
69	    v += (bos * H + i_h) * V
70	    o += (bos * H + i_h) * V
71	    h += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
72	
73	    b_o = tl.zeros([BT, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
74	    b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
75	
76	    for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
77	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
78	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
79	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
80	        # [BT, BK]
81	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
82	        # [BK, BT]
83	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
84	        # [BK, BV]
85	        b_h = tl.load(p_h, boundary_check=(0, 1))
86	
87	        # [BT, BK] @ [BK, BV] -> [BT, BV]
88	        b_o += tl.dot(b_q, b_h)
89	        # [BT, BK] @ [BK, BT] -> [BT, BT]
90	        b_A += tl.dot(b_q, b_k)
91	
92	    if USE_G:
93	        g += bos * H + i_h
94	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
95	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
96	        b_o = b_o * exp(b_g)[:, None]
97	        b_A = b_A * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :])
98	
99	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
100	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
101	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
102	        b_o = b_o * exp(b_g)[:, None]
103	        b_A = b_A * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :])
104	
105	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
106	    m_t = o_t < T
107	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] >= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
108	    b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A, 0)
109	
110	    p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
111	    p_o = tl.make_block_ptr(o, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
112	
113	    b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
114	    # to fix mma -> mma layout conversion
115	    # already solved by triton v3.2 or higher
116	    b_o = b_o * scale + tl.dot(b_A.to(b_v.dtype), b_v) * scale
117	    tl.store(p_o, b_o.to(p_o.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
118	
119	
120	@triton.heuristics({
121	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
122	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
123	    'USE_DW': lambda args: args['dw'] is not None,
124	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
125	})
126	@triton.autotune(
127	    configs=[
128	        triton.Config({}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
129	        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS
130	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
131	    ],
132	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT', 'BK', 'BV', 'USE_G', 'USE_G_GAMMA', 'USE_DW'],
133	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
134	)
135	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
136	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg(
137	    q,
138	    k,
139	    v,
140	    g,
141	    g_gamma,
142	    h,
143	    do,
144	    dh,
145	    dq,
146	    dk,
147	    dw,
148	    dv,
149	    dg,
150	    cu_seqlens,
151	    chunk_indices,
152	    scale,
153	    B: tl.constexpr,
154	    T,
155	    H: tl.constexpr,
156	    K: tl.constexpr,
157	    V: tl.constexpr,
158	    BT: tl.constexpr,
159	    BK: tl.constexpr,
160	    BV: tl.constexpr,
161	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
162	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
163	    USE_DW: tl.constexpr,
164	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
165	):
166	    i_k, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
167	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
168	
169	    all = B * T
170	    if IS_VARLEN:
171	        i_tg = i_t
172	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
173	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
174	        T = eos - bos
175	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
176	    else:
177	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
178	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
179	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
180	
181	    # offset calculation
182	    v += (bos * H + i_h) * V
183	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
184	    h += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
185	    dh += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
186	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
187	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
188	    dq += (bos * H + i_h) * K
189	    dk += (bos * H + i_h) * K
190	
191	    # for delta rule only
192	    if USE_DW:
193	        dw += (bos * H + i_h) * K
194	        dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
195	
196	    if USE_G:
197	        dg += i_k * all * H
198	        b_dg_last = tl.zeros([1], dtype=tl.float32) if USE_G else None
199	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
200	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
201	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
202	        b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
203	    b_dq = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32)
204	    b_dk = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32)
205	    b_ds = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
206	    b_dw = tl.zeros([BT, BK], dtype=tl.float32) if USE_DW else None
207	
208	    for i_v in range(tl.cdiv(V, BV)):
209	        p_v = tl.make_block_ptr(v, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
210	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
211	        p_h = tl.make_block_ptr(h, (V, K), (1, V), (i_v * BV, i_k * BK), (BV, BK), (0, 1))
212	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh, (V, K), (1, V), (i_v * BV, i_k * BK), (BV, BK), (0, 1))
213	        # [BT, BV]
214	        b_v = tl.load(p_v, boundary_check=(0, 1))
215	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
216	        # [BV, BK]
217	        b_h = tl.load(p_h, boundary_check=(0, 1))
218	        b_dh = tl.load(p_dh, boundary_check=(0, 1))
219	        if USE_G:
220	            b_dg_last += (tl.sum(b_h * b_dh))
221	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BT] -> [BT, BT]
222	        b_ds += tl.dot(b_do, tl.trans(b_v))
223	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BK] -> [BT, BK]
224	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_do, b_h.to(b_do.dtype))
225	        # [BT, BV] @ [BV, BK] -> [BT, BK]
226	        b_dk += tl.dot(b_v, b_dh.to(b_v.dtype))
227	        if USE_DW:
228	            p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
229	            b_dv = tl.load(p_dv, boundary_check=(0, 1))
230	            b_dw += tl.dot(b_dv.to(b_v.dtype), b_h.to(b_v.dtype))
231	
232	    if USE_DW:
233	        p_dw = tl.make_block_ptr(dw, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
234	        tl.store(p_dw, -b_dw.to(p_dw.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
235	
236	    tl.debug_barrier()
237	    p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
238	    p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
239	    b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
240	    b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
241	
242	    p_dq = tl.make_block_ptr(dq, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
243	    p_dk = tl.make_block_ptr(dk, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
244	
245	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
246	    m_t = o_t < T
247	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] >= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
248	    if USE_G:
249	        b_dg = tl.zeros([BT], dtype=tl.float32)
250	        g += bos * H + i_h
251	        dg += bos * H + i_h
252	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
253	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
254	        b_g_last = tl.load(g + (min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1) * H)
255	        b_dg_last *= exp(b_g_last)
256	
257	        b_dq = b_dq * exp(b_g)[:, None] * scale
258	        b_dg += tl.sum(b_dq * b_q, axis=1)
259	
260	        b_dk = b_dk * tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
261	        b_dg -= tl.sum(b_k * b_dk, axis=1)
262	        b_dg_last += tl.sum(b_dk * b_k)
263	
264	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :]), 0) * scale
265	        b_ds2 = b_ds * tl.dot(b_q, tl.trans(b_k))
266	        b_dg += tl.sum(b_ds2, axis=1)
267	        b_dg -= tl.sum(b_ds2, axis=0)
268	
269	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
270	        # [BT, BK]
271	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
272	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q)
273	        p_dg = tl.make_block_ptr(dg, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
274	        # (SY 09/21) revcumsum in a separate kernel due to strange triton compiler issue
275	        # b_dg = tl.dot(tl.where(o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :], 1., 0.), b_dg, allow_tf32=False) + b_dg_last)
276	        b_dg = tl.where(o_t < min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1, b_dg, b_dg + b_dg_last)
277	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
278	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
279	        tl.store(p_dg, b_dg.to(p_dg.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0,))
280	
281	    elif USE_G_GAMMA:
282	        b_dq = b_dq * exp(b_g)[:, None] * scale
283	        b_dk = b_dk * tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
284	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds * exp(b_g[:, None] - b_g[None, :]), 0) * scale
285	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
286	        # [BT, BK]
287	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
288	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q)
289	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
290	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
291	
292	    else:
293	        b_ds = tl.where(m_A, b_ds, 0)
294	        b_ds = b_ds.to(b_k.dtype)
295	        b_dq += tl.dot(b_ds, b_k)
296	        b_dk += tl.dot(tl.trans(b_ds), b_q) * scale
297	        b_dq *= scale
298	        tl.store(p_dq, b_dq.to(p_dq.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
299	        tl.store(p_dk, b_dk.to(p_dk.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
300	
301	
302	@triton.heuristics({
303	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
304	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
305	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
306	})
307	@triton.autotune(
308	    configs=[
309	        triton.Config({}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
310	        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS
311	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
312	    ],
313	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT', 'BK', 'BV', 'USE_G', 'USE_G_GAMMA'],
314	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
315	)
316	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
317	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dv(
318	    q,
319	    k,
320	    g,
321	    g_gamma,
322	    do,
323	    dv,
324	    dh,
325	    cu_seqlens,
326	    chunk_indices,
327	    scale,
328	    T,
329	    H: tl.constexpr,
330	    K: tl.constexpr,
331	    V: tl.constexpr,
332	    BT: tl.constexpr,
333	    BK: tl.constexpr,
334	    BV: tl.constexpr,
335	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
336	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
337	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
338	):
339	    i_v, i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1), tl.program_id(2)
340	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
341	    if IS_VARLEN:
342	        i_tg = i_t
343	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
344	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
345	        T = eos - bos
346	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
347	    else:
348	        NT = tl.cdiv(T, BT)
349	        i_tg = i_b * NT + i_t
350	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
351	
352	    b_dv = tl.zeros([BT, BV], dtype=tl.float32)
353	
354	    # offset calculation
355	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
356	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
357	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
358	    dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
359	    dh += (i_tg * H + i_h).to(tl.int64) * K*V
360	
361	    b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
362	    for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
363	        p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
364	        p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
365	        b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
366	        b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
367	        b_A += tl.dot(b_k, b_q)
368	        p_dh = tl.make_block_ptr(dh, (K, V), (V, 1), (i_k * BK, i_v * BV), (BK, BV), (1, 0))
369	        b_dh = tl.load(p_dh, boundary_check=(0, 1))
370	        b_dv += tl.dot(b_k, b_dh.to(b_k.dtype))
371	
372	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
373	    m_t = o_t < T
374	    if USE_G:
375	        g += bos * H + i_h
376	        p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
377	        b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
378	        b_g_last = tl.load(g + (min(i_t * BT + BT, T) - 1) * H)
379	    if USE_G_GAMMA:
380	        b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
381	        b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
382	        b_g_last = b_gamma * min(BT, T - i_t * BT)
383	
384	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
385	    if USE_G or USE_G_GAMMA:
386	        b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A * exp(b_g[None, :] - b_g[:, None]) * scale, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
387	        b_dv *= tl.where(m_t, exp(-b_g + b_g_last), 0)[:, None]
388	    else:
389	        b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A * scale, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
390	    p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
391	    p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
392	    b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
393	    b_dv += tl.dot(b_A.to(b_do.dtype), b_do)
394	    tl.store(p_dv, b_dv.to(p_dv.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))
395	
396	
397	@triton.heuristics({
398	    'USE_G': lambda args: args['g'] is not None,
399	    'USE_G_GAMMA': lambda args: args['g_gamma'] is not None,
400	    'USE_A': lambda args: args['A'] is not None,
401	    'IS_VARLEN': lambda args: args['cu_seqlens'] is not None,
402	})
403	@triton.autotune(
404	    configs=[
405	        triton.Config({}, num_warps=num_warps, num_stages=num_stages)
406	        for num_warps in NUM_WARPS
407	        for num_stages in [2, 3, 4]
408	    ],
409	    key=['H', 'K', 'V', 'BT', 'BK', 'BV', 'USE_G'],
410	    **autotune_cache_kwargs,
411	)
412	@triton.jit(do_not_specialize=['T'])
413	def chunk_bwd_kernel_dv_local(
414	    q,
415	    k,
416	    g,
417	    g_gamma,
418	    A,
419	    do,
420	    dv,
421	    cu_seqlens,
422	    chunk_indices,
423	    scale,
424	    T,
425	    H: tl.constexpr,
426	    K: tl.constexpr,
427	    V: tl.constexpr,
428	    BT: tl.constexpr,
429	    BK: tl.constexpr,
430	    BV: tl.constexpr,
431	    USE_G: tl.constexpr,
432	    USE_G_GAMMA: tl.constexpr,
433	    USE_A: tl.constexpr,
434	    IS_VARLEN: tl.constexpr,
435	):
436	    i_t, i_bh = tl.program_id(0), tl.program_id(1)
437	    i_b, i_h = i_bh // H, i_bh % H
438	    if IS_VARLEN:
439	        i_n, i_t = tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2).to(tl.int32), tl.load(chunk_indices + i_t * 2 + 1).to(tl.int32)
440	        bos, eos = tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n).to(tl.int32), tl.load(cu_seqlens + i_n + 1).to(tl.int32)
441	        T = eos - bos
442	    else:
443	        bos, eos = i_b * T, i_b * T + T
444	
445	    # offset calculation
446	    q += (bos * H + i_h) * K
447	    k += (bos * H + i_h) * K
448	    do += (bos * H + i_h) * V
449	    dv += (bos * H + i_h) * V
450	
451	    if USE_A:
452	        p_A = tl.make_block_ptr(A + (bos * H + i_h) * BT, (BT, T), (1, H*BT), (0, i_t * BT), (BT, BT), (0, 1))
453	        b_A = tl.load(p_A, boundary_check=(0, 1))
454	    else:
455	        if USE_G:
456	            g += bos * H + i_h
457	            p_g = tl.make_block_ptr(g, (T,), (H,), (i_t * BT,), (BT,), (0,))
458	            b_g = tl.load(p_g, boundary_check=(0,))
459	        if USE_G_GAMMA:
460	            b_gamma = tl.load(g_gamma + i_h)
461	            b_g = b_gamma * (tl.arange(0, BT) + 1)
462	
463	        b_A = tl.zeros([BT, BT], dtype=tl.float32)
464	        for i_k in range(tl.cdiv(K, BK)):
465	            p_k = tl.make_block_ptr(k, (T, K), (H*K, 1), (i_t * BT, i_k * BK), (BT, BK), (1, 0))
466	            p_q = tl.make_block_ptr(q, (K, T), (1, H*K), (i_k * BK, i_t * BT), (BK, BT), (0, 1))
467	
468	            b_k = tl.load(p_k, boundary_check=(0, 1))
469	            b_q = tl.load(p_q, boundary_check=(0, 1))
470	            b_A += tl.dot(b_k, b_q) * scale
471	        if USE_G or USE_G_GAMMA:
472	            b_A *= exp(b_g[None, :] - b_g[:, None])
473	
474	    o_t = i_t * BT + tl.arange(0, BT)
475	    m_t = o_t < T
476	    m_A = (o_t[:, None] <= o_t[None, :]) & (m_t[:, None] & m_t)
477	    b_A = tl.where(m_A, b_A, 0).to(do.dtype.element_ty)
478	
479	    for i_v in range(tl.cdiv(V, BV)):
480	        p_do = tl.make_block_ptr(do, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
481	        p_dv = tl.make_block_ptr(dv, (T, V), (H*V, 1), (i_t * BT, i_v * BV), (BT, BV), (1, 0))
482	        b_do = tl.load(p_do, boundary_check=(0, 1))
483	        b_dv = tl.dot(b_A.to(b_do.dtype), b_do)
484	        tl.store(p_dv, b_dv.to(p_dv.dtype.element_ty), boundary_check=(0, 1))

487	def chunk_fwd_o(
488	    q: torch.Tensor,
489	    k: torch.Tensor,
490	    v: torch.Tensor,
491	    h: torch.Tensor,

494	    scale: float | None = None,
495	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
496	    chunk_size: int = 64,

501	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
502	    if scale is None:
503	        scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
504	
505	    o = torch.empty_like(v)

519	        H=H,

521	        V=V,
522	        BT=BT,
523	    )

630	def chunk_bwd_dqkwg(
631	    q: torch.Tensor,
632	    k: torch.Tensor,
633	    v: torch.Tensor,
634	    do: torch.Tensor,
635	    h: torch.Tensor,
636	    dh: torch.Tensor,
637	    w: torch.Tensor | None = None,
638	    g: torch.Tensor | None = None,
639	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor | None = None,
640	    dv: torch.Tensor | None = None,
641	    scale: float | None = None,
642	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
643	    chunk_size: int = 64,
644	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
645	
646	    B, T, H, K, V = *k.shape, v.shape[-1]
647	    BT = chunk_size
648	    chunk_indices = prepare_chunk_indices(cu_seqlens, BT) if cu_seqlens is not None else None
649	    NT = triton.cdiv(T, BT) if cu_seqlens is None else len(chunk_indices)
650	
651	    CONST_TILING = 64 if check_shared_mem() else 32
652	    BK = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(K), 16), CONST_TILING)
653	    BV = min(max(triton.next_power_of_2(V), 16), CONST_TILING)
654	    NK = triton.cdiv(K, BK)
655	    dq = torch.empty_like(q)
656	    dk = torch.empty_like(k)
657	    dg = torch.empty(NK, *g.shape, dtype=torch.float32, device=g.device) if g is not None else None
658	    dw = torch.empty_like(w) if w is not None else None
659	
660	    grid = (NK, NT, B * H)
661	    chunk_bwd_kernel_dqkwg[grid](
662	        q=q,
663	        k=k,
664	        v=v,
665	        g=g,
666	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
667	        h=h,
668	        do=do,
669	        dh=dh,
670	        dw=dw,
671	        dq=dq,
672	        dk=dk,
673	        dv=dv,
674	        dg=dg,
675	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
676	        chunk_indices=chunk_indices,
677	        scale=scale,
678	        B=B,
679	        T=T,
680	        H=H,
681	        K=K,
682	        V=V,
683	        BT=BT,
684	        BK=BK,
685	        BV=BV,
686	    )
687	
688	    if dg is not None:
689	        dg = dg.sum(0)
690	    return dq, dk, dw, dg
```

```
4	import torch
5	
6	from fla.ops.common.fused_chunk import fused_chunk
7	
8	
9	def fused_chunk_simple_gla(
10	    q: torch.Tensor,
11	    k: torch.Tensor,
12	    v: torch.Tensor,
13	    g: torch.Tensor = None,
14	    g_gamma: torch.Tensor = None,
15	    scale: float | None = None,
16	    initial_state: torch.Tensor | None = None,
17	    output_final_state: bool = False,
18	    cu_seqlens: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
19	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
20	    r"""
21	    Args:
22	        q (torch.Tensor):
23	            queries of shape `[B, T, H, K]`.
24	        k (torch.Tensor):
25	            keys of shape `[B, T, H, K]`.
26	        v (torch.Tensor):
27	            values of shape `[B, T, H, V]`.
28	        g (torch.Tensor):
29	            Forget gates of shape `[B, T, H]`.
30	            Compared to GLA, the gating is head-wise instead of elementwise.
31	        g_gamma (torch.Tensor):
32	            Log decay of shape `[H]`.
33	            Head-wise data-independent decay is used if `g_gamma` is provided.
34	            Only one of `g` or `g_gamma` should be provided.
35	        scale (Optional[int]):
36	            Scale factor for the attention scores.
37	            If not provided, it will default to `1 / sqrt(K)`. Default: `None`.
38	        initial_state (Optional[torch.Tensor]):
39	            Initial state of shape `[N, H, K, V]` for `N` input sequences.
40	            For equal-length input sequences, `N` equals the batch size `B`.
41	            Default: `None`.
42	        output_final_state (Optional[bool]):
43	            Whether to output the final state of shape `[N, H, K, V]`. Default: `False`.
44	        cu_seqlens (torch.LongTensor):
45	            Cumulative sequence lengths of shape `[N+1]` used for variable-length training,
46	            consistent with the FlashAttention API.
47	
48	    Returns:
49	        o (torch.Tensor):
50	            Outputs of shape `[B, T, H, V]`.
51	        final_state (torch.Tensor):
52	            Final state of shape `[N, H, K, V]` if `output_final_state=True` else `None`.
53	
54	    Examples::
55	        >>> import torch
56	        >>> import torch.nn.functional as F
57	        >>> from einops import rearrange
58	        >>> from fla.ops.simple_gla import fused_chunk_simple_gla
59	        # inputs with equal lengths
60	        >>> B, T, H, K, V = 4, 2048, 4, 512, 512
61	        >>> q = torch.randn(B, T, H, K, device='cuda')
62	        >>> k = torch.randn(B, T, H, K, device='cuda')
63	        >>> v = torch.randn(B, T, H, V, device='cuda')
64	        >>> g = F.logsigmoid(torch.randn(B, T, H, device='cuda'))
65	        >>> h0 = torch.randn(B, H, K, V, device='cuda')
66	        >>> o, ht = fused_chunk_simple_gla(
67	            q, k, v, g,
68	            initial_state=h0,
69	            output_final_state=True
70	        )
71	        # for variable-length inputs, the batch size `B` is expected to be 1 and `cu_seqlens` is required
72	        >>> q, k, v, g = map(lambda x: rearrange(x, 'b t h d -> 1 (b t) h d'), (q, k, v, g))
73	        # for a batch with 4 sequences, `cu_seqlens` with 5 start/end positions are expected
74	        >>> cu_seqlens = q.new_tensor([0, 2048, 4096, 6144, 8192], dtype=torch.long)
75	        >>> o, ht = fused_chunk_simple_gla(
76	            q, k, v, g,
77	            initial_state=h0,
78	            output_final_state=True,
79	            cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens
80	        )
81	    """
82	    if cu_seqlens is not None:
83	        if q.shape[0] != 1:
84	            raise ValueError(
85	                f"The batch size is expected to be 1 rather than {q.shape[0]} when using `cu_seqlens`."
86	                f"Please flatten variable-length inputs before processing.",
87	            )
88	        if initial_state is not None and initial_state.shape[0] != len(cu_seqlens) - 1:
89	            raise ValueError(
90	                f"The number of initial states is expected to be equal to the number of input sequences, "
91	                f"i.e., {len(cu_seqlens) - 1} rather than {initial_state.shape[0]}.",
92	            )
93	    if scale is None:
94	        scale = k.shape[-1] ** -0.5
95	    o, final_state = fused_chunk(
96	        q=q,
97	        k=k,
98	        v=v,
99	        g=g,
100	        g_gamma=g_gamma,
101	        scale=scale,
102	        initial_state=initial_state,
103	        output_final_state=output_final_state,
104	        cu_seqlens=cu_seqlens,
105	    )
106	    return o, final_state
107
```

完美。现在做一个深度的分析报告。让我验证 chunk_size 计算逻辑和 SM120 架构的约束：

> DEVELOPER

深度调研 InfLLM-v2 sparse attention stage2 的下一步优化路径，目标是判断哪条路有真实 wall 收益。

## 背景

项目：/user_4813494d/openbmb（OpenBMB MiniCPM-SALA prefill）。8 层 standard attention（layer 0,9,16,17,22,29,30,31）都过 InfLLM-v2 sparse pipeline：stage1 算分 → max_pool → topk → stage2 sparse FA。

当前 prefill 热点（docs/prefill.md §1）：
- **extend_sparse_fa 84ms/chunk = 8 sparse layer × ~10.5ms**

生产 stage2 形态（重要！）：
- 第一个 full chunk：long causal prefill 形态
- **第二个 full chunk 起**：实际是 decode-style，每层 `q_len=8192 (展开后 expanded q)` × `topk=96 blocks × block_size=64`
- KV cache layout：page_size=1（FlashInfer 把 sparse blocks 展开成 page1 page table）
- 用 BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper

## 已落地（不要再回头）

§2.10 direct topk → FI indices: 跳过 metadata.sparse_page_table materialize/write，metadata microbench 8x，wall +3-4%。
§2.6 prefill_block_table_v3 + §2.4 direct sparse page table。

## 已否决（关键，理解为什么）

### §3.13 backend / split-KV 旋钮枯竭
- `default(auto)+TC=10.04ms` 与 `fa2+TC=9590us` 实战相当
- `fa3` / `cutlass` / `trtllm-gen` 失败或不支持 group_size=16
- `flash_attn_with_kvcache`：`Can not import FA3 in sgl_kernel`
- decode wrapper 强切 → max_diff=0 但性能持平

### §3.19 BlockSparse split + LSE merge（重点失败案例）
完整探索（备份 `outputs/prefill_experiments/stage2_blocksplit_unlanded.patch`）：
- profile：第二个 full chunk 起每层 95 full + 1 partial blocks
- `BlockSparseAttentionWrapper(R=1,C=64)` 双 head-group fwd-only 9.11ms vs page1 expanded 10.11ms（**离线 +10%**）
- BlockSparse + page64 partial + Triton log2-LSE merge 单 head 4.74→4.95ms（+4%），cos=0.99999857 max_diff=0.000488
- LSE 是 log2 域必须 `2**(lse-max_lse)` 不是 exp
- gate 唯一可用：`metadata.token_pos_in_bs.min() >= topk_tokens`
- 线上接入 `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE2_BLOCKSPLIT=1`：第二 full chunk 命中 split，**extend_sparse_fa 没有下降**（118.6ms / 后续 82-84ms），no-profile 15chunk avg 7.114s vs default 7.110s

文档结论：**离线 +4% forward-only 没穿透 wall，q_h.contiguous() / 双 wrapper / merge 开销吃光收益。同构 split 接入路线封死，重启必须换 KV/block layout 或单 wrapper / 单 kernel 的完整等价实现。**

### §3.20 page64 双 wrapper
- page1 → page64 metadata 384MB → 6MB，fwd 10.04→8.97ms 离线
- 双 wrapper 破坏 single-wrapper plan-cache，每层 bf 多 18-19ms
- 加 `SGLANG_MINICPM_PAGE64_PLAN_CACHE=1` microbench OK，**真实 15chunk 与 page1 端到端打平**
- 文档原文："stage2 真正剩余的算法/layout 路径：单 wrapper KV layout 或 custom block-sparse stage2 kernel"

### §3.21 Triton 直写
生产形态 triton_direct=10.37ms vs FlashInfer 10.13ms 慢 2.4%；tile sweep 仍慢 18%

## 你的任务

### 第 1 步：读源码确认 FlashInfer 当前路径

请读：
1. `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_attention_kernels.py` —— 找 stage2 的 FlashInfer wrapper 调用、plan/forward 流程
2. `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_sparse_utils.py` —— 找 stage2 入口（topk_idx → wrapper.run）

### 第 2 步：看 FlashInfer 是否原生支持 block-sparse paged KV

在 venv 找 FlashInfer 版本和源码：
```
find /opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env -path "*flashinfer*" -name "*.py" | head -20
```
特别关心：
- `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper` 是否原生支持 `page_size>1` + sparse `kv_indices`？
- 有没有 `BlockSparsePagedKVAttentionWrapper` 之类直接吃 block-aligned topk 的 API？
- FA3 在当前 FlashInfer 0.6.8.post1[cu13] 是 disabled 还是 importable？查 sgl_kernel `Can not import FA3`：
  ```
  find /opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env -path "*sgl_kernel*flash*" 2>/dev/null
  ```

### 第 3 步：评估三条候选

A. **single-wrapper page64 KV cache backing layout**：把 KV cache 在 backing storage 就是 page_size=64 存，不需要把 sparse blocks 展开成 page1。改造 SGLang KV cache 分配 + page table 计算。
B. **写一个 fused stage2 kernel**（直接吃 topk_idx + 原始 KV cache，不通过 paged wrapper）。
C. **stage2 改用 FA3 native sparse** 如果 FlashInfer 0.6.8 已支持。

每条给：
- 工程量（小时/天/周）
- 收益上限（用 Triton 18% 慢、page64 离线 -10% 当上限）
- 真正 blocker（KV cache 整 SGLang 重做 / kernel 等价性 / FA3 import 失败原因）

### 第 4 步：我要的结论（≤ 500 字）

1. **8 层 sparse 的 wall 占比真的是 84/470=18%，但每层 10.5ms 中 fwd 真正占多少 vs plan/metadata？** 给一个分解。
2. **page64 双 wrapper 失败的根因到底是 plan 开销还是 wrapper 切换**？看代码判断单 wrapper page64 的可行性。
3. **三条候选哪条最值得做？** 按 (收益 × 成功概率 / 工程量) 排序。
4. **如果是 A（KV cache layout 重做），SGLang 的 KV cache 分配在哪里？** 给文件路径+函数名，让我能立刻评估改动 surface。

直接给可判断结论。

n_block*64 是 64 的倍数\")\nprint(\"  - pool stride=4，所以 pool 的输出索引 k 覆盖原始 [k*4-1, k*4+3] 范围\")\nprint(\"  - 32x64 trait：每 block 处理 64 个原始 K，pool 则需要跨越多个 K 块\")\nprint(\"  => 如果只 fuse pool 到 stage1 epilogue，tile boundary 无法对齐\")\nprint(\"  => 需要缓冲一个 tile 的中间结果，或延迟计算到 pool 内核\")\n\nprint(\"\\nOBSTACLE 2：Causal Mask 边界\")\nprint(\"  stage1 kernel 在 compute_mma 过程中应用 causal mask\")\nprint(\"  mask 基于 q_idx 和 k_idx（原始索引）\")\nprint(\"  pool kernel 再次基于 q_block 和 k_block 应用 causal mask（block-level）\")\nprint(\"  fuse 到 epilogue 时，需同时维护两级 mask 状态\")\nprint(\"  => 算法/数值问题，non-trivial\")\n\nprint(\"\\nOBSTACLE 3：Topk 难以同步 Fuse\")\nprint(\"  bitonic_topk kernel 当前输入 [num_heads, q, num_blocks ≈ 2048]\")\nprint(\"  输出 [num_heads, q, topk=96]\")\nprint(\"  若要在 stage1 grid 内做 topk，需改 grid 配置（当前 grid=(num_m_block, num_h)）\")\nprint(\"  topk 通常用 1 block per q-row（或多块合并），配置复杂\")\nprint(\"  stage1 已用 split-KV 并行化，并行度难调整\")\nprint(\"  => 工程问题，需大量重构\")\n\nprint(\"\\n4. 只 Fuse Pool 的成本收益\")\nprint(\"-\" * 50)\n\n# stage1 kernel 输出：score 从 registers 写到 HBM\n# 时间：stage1 10.38ms（含输出），占比约 5-10%\n# pool kernel 启动 + 计算：~2-3ms\n# 若 fuse：省掉 pool kernel 启动，节省约 0.1-0.5ms HBM roundtrip\n\nprint(\"当前分解:\")\nprint(\"  - stage1 输出 score：~0.5-1ms（HBM 写）\")\nprint(\"  - pool kernel 启动+执行：~2-3ms（score 读 + block_score 写）\")\nprint(\"  - topk kernel 启动+执行：~0.5-1ms（block_score 读 + indices 写）\")\n\nprint(\"\\n只 fuse pool 的效果：\")\nprint(\"  - 省 pool kernel 启动（~0.1ms）\")\nprint(\"  - 省 score->HBM->pool 的 roundtrip（~1-2ms，取决于 kernel 延迟隐藏）\")\nprint(\"  - 总计：~1-2ms/layer = 8-16ms/8layers ≈ 12-20% 加速\")\nprint(\"  但需要：\")\nprint(\"  - epilogue tiling/缓冲复杂\")\nprint(\"  - 寄存器压力（多个 pool tile 的中间态）\")\nprint(\"  - block boundary 对齐逻辑\")\n\nprint(\"\\n5. 真实拦路石排序（优先级）\")\nprint(\"-\" * 50)\n\nprint(\"\\n[1] OBSTACLE - 算法/数值问题（基本无解）\")\nprint(\"    - 两级 causal mask 的 fusion 语义复杂：\")\nprint(\"      block-level pool 需跨越多个原始 K tile\")\nprint(\"      要保证 stage1+pool 联合后的 causal mask 等价很难\")\nprint(\"    - 预估难度：LOW，纯工程\")\n\nprint(\"\\n6. 建议方案\")\nprint(\"-\" * 50)\nprint(\"不做 fusion。理由：\")\nprint(\"  1. 矛盾的对齐需求：\")\nprint(\"     - stage1 output shape (kBlockM/16, kBlockN) 与 pool stride 不兼容\")\nprint(\"     - tool_result Bash
```
=== Stage1 Epilogue Fusion 可行性分析 ===

1. 当前 Stage1 Epilogue 数据写出量
--------------------------------------------------
Stage1 score 输出形状：(2, 8192, 131072)
Stage1 score 输出大小：4.295 GB
Per KV head：2.147 GB

2. Max-pooling 核心数据流
--------------------------------------------------
Pool input (stage1 score)：[2, 131072, 8192]
  = 4.295 GB
Pool output (block scores)：[2, 131072, 2048]
  = 1.074 GB

3. Fusion 拦路石分析
--------------------------------------------------

问题 1：共享内存和寄存器预算
  hdim16_reduce 在 stage1 epilogue 用 register 缓存累加器，输出 4 thread x 64 elem
  若要做 in-register 4-element max，需额外 4 float32 per thread ≈ 16 bytes/thread
  64 threads/warp x 2 warp = 128 threads：+ 2KB shared mem 或 per-thread regs
  当前 stage1 kernel smem size 已配置，epilogue 还有余量（FA kernel 典型 48-96KB）
  => 可行，预算充足

OBSTACLE 1：Stride/Block Boundary Mismatch
  stage1 gP shape = Shape<kBlockM/16, kBlockN> = (2, 64)
  stage1 在 kBlockN=64 的粒度上推进循环，每次写出 64 个 K index 的 score
  pool kernel 参数：kernel_size=5, stride=4
  ==> 5 个连续元素 max 后每隔 4 个取一个
  当 n_block 边界与 pool kernel size/stride 不对齐时：
  - n_block*64 是 64 的倍数
  - pool stride=4，所以 pool 的输出索引 k 覆盖原始 [k*4-1, k*4+3] 范围
  - 32x64 trait：每 block 处理 64 个原始 K，pool 则需要跨越多个 K 块
  => 如果只 fuse pool 到 stage1 epilogue，tile boundary 无法对齐
  => 需要缓冲一个 tile 的中间结果，或延迟计算到 pool 内核

OBSTACLE 2：Causal Mask 边界
  stage1 kernel 在 compute_mma 过程中应用 causal mask
  mask 基于 q_idx 和 k_idx（原始索引）
  pool kernel 再次基于 q_block 和 k_block 应用 causal mask（block-level）
  fuse 到 epilogue 时，需同时维护两级 mask 状态
  => 算法/数值问题，non-trivial

OBSTACLE 3：Topk 难以同步 Fuse
  bitonic_topk kernel 当前输入 [num_heads, q, num_blocks ≈ 2048]
  输出 [num_heads, q, topk=96]
  若要在 stage1 grid 内做 topk，需改 grid 配置（当前 grid=(num_m_block, num_h)）
  topk 通常用 1 block per q-row（或多块合并），配置复杂
  stage1 已用 split-KV 并行化，并行度难调整
  => 工程问题，需大量重构

4. 只 Fuse Pool 的成本收益
--------------------------------------------------
当前分解:
  - stage1 输出 score：~0.5-1ms（HBM 写）
  - pool kernel 启动+执行：~2-3ms（score 读 + block_score 写）
  - topk kernel 启动+执行：~0.5-1ms（block_score 读 + indices 写）

只 fuse pool 的效果：
  - 省 pool kernel 启动（~0.1ms）
  - 省 score->HBM->pool 的 roundtrip（~1-2ms，取决于 kernel 延迟隐藏）
  - 总计：~1-2ms/layer = 8-16ms/8layers ≈ 12-20% 加速
  但需要：
  - epilogue tiling/缓冲复杂
  - 寄存器压力（多个 pool tile 的中间态）
  - block boundary 对齐逻辑

5. 真实拦路石排序（优先级）
--------------------------------------------------

[1] OBSTACLE - 算法/数值问题（基本无解）
    - 两级 causal mask 的 fusion 语义复杂：
      block-level pool 需跨越多个原始 K tile
      要保证 stage1+pool 联合后的 causal mask 等价很难
    - 预估难度：HIGH，需形式化验证

[2] OBSTACLE - 工程问题（可解但成本大）
    - Stride/block boundary 对齐：
      stage1 tile (kBlockM/16 x 64) vs pool stride (4)
      需要缓存一个滑动窗口的中间结果，或改 kernel loop
    - Topk 融合需改 grid 配置和并行化策略
    - 预估难度：MEDIUM-HIGH，改动量 ~500-1000 loc

[3] WORKING - 共享内存/寄存器预算
    - stage1 kernel 的 epilogue 还有余量
    - in-register max(4) + smem buffer 可行
    - 预估难度：LOW，纯工程

6. 建议方案
--------------------------------------------------
不做 fusion。理由：
  1. 矛盾的对齐需求：
     - stage1 output shape (kBlockM/16, kBlockN) 与 pool stride 不兼容
     - block boundary 对齐需特殊处理，失去 register blocking 的好处
  2. causal mask 两级语义很难保证等价
  3. 预期收益仅 1-2ms/layer（总耗时 10ms），ROI 差
  4. 风险：数值不一致会导致 topk 错误（致命）

替代方案：
  - 向 InfLLM 官方提交 fast path request（blocksize=64 特化 kernel）
  - 或改写 pool+topk 为单个 fused kernel（不融 stage1）
```

```
37	_MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL = max(

39	)

52	)
53	_MINICPM_PROFILE_TOTALS: dict[str, float] = {}
54	_MINICPM_PROFILE_COUNTS: dict[str, int] = {}
55	
56	
57	def _profile_can_sync() -> bool:
58	    if not _MINICPM_PROFILE:
59	        return False
60	    try:
61	        return not torch.cuda.is_current_stream_capturing()
62	    except RuntimeError:
63	        return False
64	
65	
66	def _profile_begin() -> float:
67	    if _profile_can_sync():
68	        torch.cuda.synchronize()
69	    return time.perf_counter()
70	
71	
72	def _profile_end(start: float, bucket: str):
73	    if not _MINICPM_PROFILE:
74	        return
75	    if _profile_can_sync():
76	        torch.cuda.synchronize()
77	    _MINICPM_PROFILE_TOTALS[bucket] = _MINICPM_PROFILE_TOTALS.get(bucket, 0.0) + (
78	        time.perf_counter() - start
79	    ) * 1000.0
80	
81	
82	def _profile_step(counter: str, buckets: tuple[str, ...], label: str):
83	    if not _MINICPM_PROFILE:
84	        return
85	    count = _MINICPM_PROFILE_COUNTS.get(counter, 0) + 1
86	    _MINICPM_PROFILE_COUNTS[counter] = count
87	    if count % _MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL != 0:
88	        return
89	    stats = ", ".join(
90	        f"{bucket}={_MINICPM_PROFILE_TOTALS.get(bucket, 0.0) / count:.3f}ms"
91	        for bucket in buckets
92	    )
93	    print(f"[minicpm-profile] {label} calls={count}, {stats}")
94

97	class AttentionParams:
98	    """Parameters for attention computation.
99	
100	    This dataclass contains all parameters needed for both flash attention
101	    and flashinfer backends.
102	    """
103	
104	    # Query, key, value tensors
105	    q: torch.Tensor

115	    cu_seqlens_k_new: torch.Tensor

122	    softcap: float = 0.0
123	
124	    # Quantization

126	    v_descale: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
127	
128	    # Other parameters

135	    flashinfer_kv_last_page_len: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
136

147

156	class AttentionKernel(ABC):
157	    """Abstract base class for attention kernels.
158	
159	    This class defines the interface that all attention kernels must implement.
160	    """
161	
162	    @abstractmethod
163	    def forward(
164	        self,

166	        layer: RadixAttention,
167	    ) -> torch.Tensor:

169	
170	        Args:
171	            params: Attention parameters

175	            Attention output tensor
176	        """
177	        pass
178	
179	    @abstractmethod
180	    def init_metadata(
181	        self,
182	        forward_batch,
183	        layer,
184	    ) -> Optional[object]:
185	        """Initialize backend-specific metadata.
186	
187	        Args:

190	
191	        Returns:
192	            Backend-specific metadata object, or None if not needed
193	        """

197	class FlashAttentionKernel(AttentionKernel):
198	    """Flash Attention kernel implementation.
```
