200	    This class wraps the flash_attn_with_kvcache function from sgl_kernel.
201	    """
202	
203	    def __init__(self):
204	        from sgl_kernel.flash_attn import flash_attn_with_kvcache
205	
206	        self.flash_attn_func = flash_attn_with_kvcache
207	
208	    def forward(
209	        self,
210	        params: AttentionParams,
211	        layer: RadixAttention,
212	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
213	        """Perform attention computation using flash attention."""
214	        # Prepare kwargs based on fa_impl_ver
215	        kwargs = {}
216	        if params.fa_impl_ver != 3:
217	            kwargs["ver"] = params.fa_impl_ver
218	
219	        return self.flash_attn_func(
220	            q=params.q,
221	            k_cache=params.k_cache,
222	            v_cache=params.v_cache,
223	            page_table=params.page_table,
224	            cache_seqlens=params.cache_seqlens,
225	            cu_seqlens_q=params.cu_seqlens_q,
226	            cu_seqlens_k_new=params.cu_seqlens_k_new,
227	            max_seqlen_q=params.max_seqlen_q,
228	            softmax_scale=params.softmax_scale,
229	            causal=params.causal,
230	            window_size=params.window_size,
231	            softcap=params.softcap,
232	            k_descale=params.k_descale,
233	            v_descale=params.v_descale,
234	            return_softmax_lse=False,
235	            num_splits=params.num_splits,
236	            **kwargs,
237	        )
238	
239	    def init_metadata(
240	        self,
241	        forward_batch,
242	        layer,
243	    ) -> Optional[object]:
244	        """Flash attention doesn't need special metadata initialization."""
245	        return None
246	
247	
248	class FlashInferKernel(AttentionKernel):
249	    """FlashInfer kernel implementation.
250	
251	    This class wraps the flashinfer attention wrappers.
252	    """
253	
254	    def __init__(self, model_runner):
255	        self.device = model_runner.device
256	        self.page_size = model_runner.page_size
257	
258	        # KV cache attributes
259	        self.kv_cache_dtype = model_runner.kv_cache_dtype
260	        self.data_type = self.kv_cache_dtype
261	
262	        # Model config attributes
263	        self.num_qo_heads = model_runner.model_config.num_attention_heads
264	        self.num_kv_heads = model_runner.model_config.get_num_kv_heads(
265	            get_tensor_model_parallel_world_size()
266	        )
267	        self.head_dim = model_runner.model_config.head_dim
268	        # Query/output data type must stay in compute precision (bf16/fp16);
269	        # only KV cache storage uses the quantized dtype (e.g. fp8_e5m2).
270	        # FlashInfer infers output dtype from q_data_type, and FP8 output
271	        # is not supported in FA2/FA3 backends.
272	        self.q_data_type = model_runner.dtype
273	
274	        # Create workspace buffers for flashinfer
275	        workspace_size = envs.SGLANG_FLASHINFER_WORKSPACE_SIZE.get()
276	        self.decode_workspace = torch.empty(
277	            workspace_size,
278	            dtype=torch.uint8,
279	            device=self.device,
280	        )
281	        self.prefill_workspace = torch.empty(
282	            workspace_size,
283	            dtype=torch.uint8,
284	            device=self.device,
285	        )
286	
287	        # Data format for flashinfer (Num heads, Head dim, Seq length)
288	        self.kv_layout = "NHD"
289	
290	        # Wrappers will be created lazily
291	        self.decode_wrapper: Optional[BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper] = None
292	        self.prefill_wrapper: Optional[BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper] = None
293	
294	        # Track if wrappers have been pre-planned for CUDA graph
295	        self.decode_wrapper_planned = False
296	        self.prefill_wrapper_planned = False
297	
298	        # Plan cache: skip wrapper.begin_forward when the decode wrapper's plan
299	        # inputs (bs, num_q/kv_heads, max_sparse_tokens, dtypes) are unchanged
300	        # across the 8 standard-attention layers of a single forward pass.
301	        # Why: flashinfer plan() takes ~23ms of CPU time per call independent
302	        # of GPU state; MiniCPM calls it 8×/chunk → 2.9s/128K prefill baseline.
303	        # kv_indptr/kv_last_page_len are structurally identical across layers
304	        # (fixed topk, page_size=1). Only kv_indices *content* changes per layer.
305	        self._plan_cache_key = None
306	        self._plan_last_layer_id = None
307	        self._plan_cached_indptr_bytes = None
308	        self._plan_cached_lpl_bytes = None
309	        self._plan_hit_count = 0
310	        self._plan_miss_count = 0
311	        self._plan_cross_hit_count = 0
312	        self._plan_cache_disable = os.getenv(
313	            "SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE", "1"
314	        ) == "0"
315	
316	        # The all-sparse prefill page table is the same tensor across the 8
317	        # standard-attention layers in one forward; only layer_id advances.
318	        self._conv_cache_pt_ptr: Optional[int] = None
319	        self._conv_cache_last_layer_id: Optional[int] = None
320	        self._conv_cached_indptr: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
321	        self._conv_cached_indices: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
322	        self._conv_cached_lpl: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
323	        self._conv_cached_pt_snapshot: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
324	        self._conv_cache_check_count = 0
325	        self._direct_fi_indptr_buf: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
326	        self._direct_fi_indices_buf: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
327	        self._direct_fi_lpl_buf: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
328	
329	    def _get_direct_fi_buffers(
330	        self,
331	        bs: int,
332	        max_sparse_tokens: int,
333	        device: torch.device,
334	    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
335	        total_indices = bs * max_sparse_tokens
336	        if (
337	            self._direct_fi_indptr_buf is None
338	            or self._direct_fi_indptr_buf.numel() < bs + 1
339	            or self._direct_fi_indptr_buf.device != device
340	        ):
341	            self._direct_fi_indptr_buf = torch.empty(
342	                bs + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
343	            )
344	        if (
345	            self._direct_fi_indices_buf is None
346	            or self._direct_fi_indices_buf.numel() < total_indices
347	            or self._direct_fi_indices_buf.device != device
348	        ):
349	            self._direct_fi_indices_buf = torch.empty(
350	                total_indices, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
351	            )
352	        if (
353	            self._direct_fi_lpl_buf is None
354	            or self._direct_fi_lpl_buf.numel() < bs
355	            or self._direct_fi_lpl_buf.device != device
356	        ):
357	            self._direct_fi_lpl_buf = torch.empty(
358	                bs, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
359	            )
360	        return (
361	            self._direct_fi_indptr_buf[: bs + 1],
362	            self._direct_fi_indices_buf[:total_indices],
363	            self._direct_fi_lpl_buf[:bs],
364	        )
365	
366	    def _get_or_create_decode_wrapper(
367	        self,
368	    ) -> "BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper":
369	        """Get or create the decode wrapper."""
370	        if self.decode_wrapper is None:
371	            from flashinfer import BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper
372	
373	            # NOTE: use_tensor_cores=True is required for num_kv_heads=1 to avoid plan_info=None bug
374	            self.decode_wrapper = BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper(
375	                self.decode_workspace,
376	                self.kv_layout,
377	                use_tensor_cores=True,
378	            )
379	        return self.decode_wrapper
380	
381	    def _get_or_create_prefill_wrapper(
382	        self,
383	    ) -> "BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper":
384	        """Get or create the prefill wrapper."""
385	        if self.prefill_wrapper is None:
386	            from flashinfer import BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper
387	
388	            self.prefill_wrapper = BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper(
389	                self.prefill_workspace,
390	                self.kv_layout,
391	                backend="fa2",
392	            )
393	        return self.prefill_wrapper
394	
395	    def plan_decode_wrapper(
396	        self,
397	        kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
398	        kv_indices: torch.Tensor,
399	        kv_last_page_len: torch.Tensor,
400	    ) -> None:
401	        """Pre-plan the decode wrapper for CUDA graph compatibility.
402	
403	        This method calls begin_forward with the given metadata to set up
404	        the flashinfer plan without calling forward. This must be called
405	        before CUDA graph capture.
406	
407	        Args:
408	            kv_indptr: Indptr array for the sparse indices
409	            kv_indices: Flattened page indices
410	            kv_last_page_len: Last page length for each sequence
411	        """
412	        self.decode_wrapper_planned = False
413	        self._get_or_create_decode_wrapper()
414	        self.decode_wrapper.begin_forward(
415	            kv_indptr,
416	            kv_indices,
417	            kv_last_page_len,
418	            self.num_qo_heads,
419	            self.num_kv_heads,
420	            self.head_dim,
421	            self.page_size,
422	            q_data_type=self.q_data_type,
423	            kv_data_type=self.data_type,
424	            non_blocking=True,
425	        )
426	        self.decode_wrapper_planned = True
427	
428	    def plan_prefill_wrapper(
429	        self,
430	        qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,
431	        kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
432	        kv_indices: torch.Tensor,
433	        kv_last_page_len: torch.Tensor,
434	        causal: bool,
435	    ) -> None:
436	        """Pre-plan the prefill wrapper for CUDA graph compatibility.
437	
438	        This method calls begin_forward with the given metadata to set up
439	        the flashinfer plan without calling forward. This must be called
440	        before CUDA graph capture.
441	
442	        Args:
443	            qo_indptr: Query indptr
444	            kv_indptr: Indptr array for the sparse indices
445	            kv_indices: Flattened page indices
446	            kv_last_page_len: Last page length for each sequence
447	            causal: Whether to apply causal masking
448	        """
449	        self.prefill_wrapper_planned = False
450	        self._get_or_create_prefill_wrapper()
451	        self.prefill_wrapper.begin_forward(
452	            qo_indptr,
453	            kv_indptr,
454	            kv_indices,
455	            kv_last_page_len,
456	            self.num_qo_heads,
457	            self.num_kv_heads,
458	            self.head_dim,
459	            self.page_size,
460	            q_data_type=self.q_data_type,
461	            kv_data_type=self.data_type,
462	            non_blocking=True,
463	            causal=causal,
464	        )
465	        self.prefill_wrapper_planned = True
466	
467	    def forward(
468	        self,
469	        params: AttentionParams,
470	        layer: RadixAttention,
471	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
472	        """Perform attention computation using flashinfer."""
473	        # Determine if this is prefill or decode based on max_seqlen_q
474	        is_prefill = params.max_seqlen_q > 1
475	        if _MINICPM_PROFILE and is_prefill:
476	            global _FI_FORWARD_PREFILL_DBG
477	            try:
478	                _FI_FORWARD_PREFILL_DBG += 1
479	            except NameError:
480	                _FI_FORWARD_PREFILL_DBG = 1
481	            if _FI_FORWARD_PREFILL_DBG <= 8:
482	                print(f"[minicpm-profile-debug] FI.forward prefill #{_FI_FORWARD_PREFILL_DBG} max_q={params.max_seqlen_q} dec_wrap={params.decode_wrapper is not None}")
483	
484	        # CUDA graph mode: use the pre-configured wrapper from params
485	        if params.decode_wrapper is not None and not is_prefill:
486	            wrapper = params.decode_wrapper
487	            using_preconverted = params.flashinfer_kv_indptr is not None
488	            if not using_preconverted:
489	                # For CUDA graph mode, update wrapper's internal buffers
490	                # Convert sparse_page_table to flashinfer format
491	                bs = params.cache_seqlens.shape[0]
492	                max_sparse_tokens = params.page_table.shape[1]
493	
494	                # Get wrapper's internal buffer shapes to avoid reallocation
495	                kv_indptr_shape = wrapper._paged_kv_indptr_buf.shape
496	                kv_indices_shape = wrapper._paged_kv_indices_buf.shape
497	                kv_last_page_len_shape = wrapper._paged_kv_last_page_len_buf.shape
498	
499	                # Create temporary buffers for conversion (reuse if possible)
500	                if (
501	                    kv_indptr_shape[0] >= bs + 1
502	                    and kv_indices_shape[0] >= bs * max_sparse_tokens
503	                    and kv_last_page_len_shape[0] >= bs
504	                ):
505	                    # Use wrapper's internal buffers directly (no allocation)
506	                    kv_indptr = wrapper._paged_kv_indptr_buf
507	                    kv_indices = wrapper._paged_kv_indices_buf
508	                    kv_last_page_len = wrapper._paged_kv_last_page_len_buf
509	                else:
510	                    # Allocate new buffers if wrapper's buffers are too small
511	                    kv_indptr = torch.zeros(
512	                        bs + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=params.page_table.device
513	                    )
514	                    kv_indices = torch.zeros(
515	                        bs * max_sparse_tokens,
516	                        dtype=torch.int32,
517	                        device=params.page_table.device,
518	                    )
519	                    kv_last_page_len = torch.zeros(
520	                        bs, dtype=torch.int32, device=params.page_table.device
521	                    )
522	
523	                start = _profile_begin()
524	                convert_sparse_page_table_to_flashinfer(
525	                    params.page_table,
526	                    params.cache_seqlens,
527	                    kv_indptr,
528	                    kv_indices,
529	                    kv_last_page_len,
530	                )
531	                _profile_end(start, "graph_decode_convert_ms")
532	        else:
533	            # Non-CUDA graph mode: create wrapper and convert on-the-fly
534	            if _MINICPM_NVTX:
535	                torch.cuda.nvtx.range_push(f"fi_convert_L{getattr(layer, 'layer_id', -1)}")
536	            _conv_start = _profile_begin()
537	            if is_prefill:
538	                wrapper = self._get_or_create_prefill_wrapper()
539	            else:
540	                wrapper = self._get_or_create_decode_wrapper()
541	
542	            # Convert page table format for flashinfer
543	            # Use pre-converted tensors if available (for CUDA graph compatibility),
544	            # otherwise convert on-the-fly (for non-CUDA graph mode)
545	            if (
546	                params.flashinfer_kv_indptr is not None
547	                and params.flashinfer_kv_indices is not None
548	                and params.flashinfer_kv_last_page_len is not None
549	            ):
550	                # Use pre-converted tensors (CUDA graph safe)
551	                kv_indptr = params.flashinfer_kv_indptr
552	                kv_indices = params.flashinfer_kv_indices
553	                kv_last_page_len = params.flashinfer_kv_last_page_len
554	            else:
555	                bs = params.cache_seqlens.shape[0]
556	                using_direct_topk_indices = (
557	                    params.flashinfer_topk_idx is not None
558	                    and params.flashinfer_base_page_table is not None
559	                    and params.flashinfer_token_to_bs is not None
560	                    and params.flashinfer_token_pos is not None
561	                    and params.flashinfer_seqlen_k is not None
562	                )
563	                if using_direct_topk_indices:
564	                    max_sparse_tokens = int(params.flashinfer_topk_idx.shape[2]) * 64
565	                else:
566	                    max_sparse_tokens = params.page_table.shape[1]
567	                cur_layer_id = getattr(layer, "layer_id", -1)
568	                page_table_ptr = (
569	                    params.flashinfer_topk_idx.data_ptr()
570	                    if using_direct_topk_indices
571	                    else params.page_table.data_ptr()
572	                )
573	
574	                conv_hit = (
575	                    _MINICPM_UNSAFE_FI_CONVERT_CACHE
576	                    and not using_direct_topk_indices
577	                    and page_table_ptr == self._conv_cache_pt_ptr
578	                    and self._conv_cache_last_layer_id is not None
579	                    and cur_layer_id > self._conv_cache_last_layer_id
580	                    and self._conv_cached_indptr is not None
581	                )
582	                if conv_hit:
583	                    if _MINICPM_CHECK_CONV_CACHE and self._conv_cache_check_count < 32:
584	                        self._conv_cache_check_count += 1
585	                        if self._conv_cached_pt_snapshot is not None:
586	                            torch.cuda.synchronize()
587	                            mismatch = int(
588	                                (
589	                                    self._conv_cached_pt_snapshot
590	                                    != params.page_table
591	                                )
592	                                .sum()
593	                                .item()
594	                            )
595	                            print(
596	                                f"[minicpm-conv-cache-check] L{cur_layer_id} "
597	                                f"mismatch={mismatch}",
598	                                flush=True,
599	                            )
600	                    kv_indptr = self._conv_cached_indptr
601	                    kv_indices = self._conv_cached_indices
602	                    kv_last_page_len = self._conv_cached_lpl
603	                else:
604	                    if using_direct_topk_indices:
605	                        _direct_buf_start = _profile_begin()
606	                        kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len = (
607	                            self._get_direct_fi_buffers(
608	                                bs,
609	                                max_sparse_tokens,
610	                                params.q.device,
611	                            )
612	                        )
613	                        _profile_end(_direct_buf_start, "fi_direct_buffer_ms")
614	                        kv_indptr[0] = 0
615	                        kv_indptr[1:] = torch.cumsum(params.cache_seqlens, dim=0)
616	                        _direct_meta_start = _profile_begin()
617	                        kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len = (
618	                            triton_topk_to_flashinfer_indices(
619	                                params.flashinfer_topk_idx,
620	                                params.flashinfer_base_page_table,
621	                                params.flashinfer_token_to_bs,
622	                                params.flashinfer_token_pos,
623	                                params.flashinfer_seqlen_k,
624	                                kv_indptr,
625	                                kv_indices,
626	                                kv_last_page_len,
627	                            )
628	                        )
629	                        _profile_end(_direct_meta_start, "fi_direct_topk_indices_ms")
630	                    else:
631	                        kv_indptr = torch.zeros(
632	                            bs + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=params.q.device
633	                        )
634	                        kv_indices = torch.zeros(
635	                            bs * max_sparse_tokens,
636	                            dtype=torch.int32,
637	                            device=params.q.device,
638	                        )
639	                        kv_last_page_len = torch.zeros(
640	                            bs, dtype=torch.int32, device=params.q.device
641	                        )
642	                        kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len = (
643	                            convert_sparse_page_table_to_flashinfer(
644	                                params.page_table,
645	                                params.cache_seqlens,
646	                                kv_indptr,
647	                                kv_indices,
648	                                kv_last_page_len,
649	                            )
650	                        )
651	                    self._conv_cache_pt_ptr = page_table_ptr
652	                    self._conv_cached_indptr = kv_indptr
653	                    self._conv_cached_indices = kv_indices
654	                    self._conv_cached_lpl = kv_last_page_len
655	                    if _MINICPM_CHECK_CONV_CACHE:
656	                        self._conv_cached_pt_snapshot = params.page_table.clone()
657	                self._conv_cache_last_layer_id = cur_layer_id
658	            _profile_end(_conv_start, "fi_convert_ms")
659	            if _MINICPM_NVTX:
660	                torch.cuda.nvtx.range_pop()
661	                torch.cuda.nvtx.range_push(f"fi_begin_forward_L{getattr(layer, 'layer_id', -1)}")
662	
663	            # Call begin_forward to set up the attention plan
664	            # This is required by flashinfer to cache data types and metadata
665	            # Skip only if we're using pre-converted tensors that match the plan
666	            using_preconverted = params.flashinfer_kv_indptr is not None
667	            _begin_start = _profile_begin()
668	            if is_prefill:
669	                if not using_preconverted:
670	                    # Prefill wrapper requires qo_indptr (query indptr)
671	                    qo_indptr = params.cu_seqlens_q
672	                    wrapper.begin_forward(
673	                        qo_indptr,
674	                        kv_indptr,
675	                        kv_indices,
676	                        kv_last_page_len,
677	                        self.num_qo_heads,
678	                        self.num_kv_heads,
679	                        self.head_dim,
680	                        self.page_size,
681	                        q_data_type=self.q_data_type,
682	                        kv_data_type=self.data_type,
683	                        non_blocking=True,
684	                        causal=params.causal,
685	                    )
686	            else:
687	                if not using_preconverted:
688	                    # Decode wrapper uses indptr, indices.
689	                    # Use actual per-sequence head counts from tensors: forward_decode
690	                    # applies a head-group split (tp_q_head_num//2, tp_kv_head_num//2)
691	                    # before calling here, so self.num_qo_heads/num_kv_heads are wrong.
692	                    cur_layer_id = getattr(layer, "layer_id", -1)
693	                    cache_key = (
694	                        kv_indptr.shape[0],
695	                        kv_indices.shape[0],
696	                        kv_last_page_len.shape[0],
697	                        int(params.q.shape[1]),
698	                        int(params.k_cache.shape[2]),
699	                    )
700	                    # Within-forward fast path: monotonically increasing layer_id
701	                    # + same cache_key means plan is guaranteed valid (same
702	                    # batch/sparse layout, verified at forward construction).
703	                    reuse_plan = (
704	                        (not self._plan_cache_disable)
705	                        and self._plan_cache_key == cache_key
706	                        and self._plan_last_layer_id is not None
707	                        and cur_layer_id > self._plan_last_layer_id
708	                    )
709	                    # Cross-forward (chunk-boundary) reuse: layer_id wraps to L0.
710	                    # shape_only_plan_cache trusts the shape key for all-sparse
711	                    # batches; byte-compare fallback for mixed batches.
712	                    _cross_forward = False
713	                    if (
714	                        not reuse_plan
715	                        and (not self._plan_cache_disable)
716	                        and (not _MINICPM_DISABLE_CROSS_CHUNK_PLAN_CACHE)
717	                        and self._plan_cache_key == cache_key
718	                    ):
719	                        if params.shape_only_plan_cache:
720	                            reuse_plan = True
721	                            _cross_forward = True
722	                            self._plan_cross_hit_count += 1
723	                        elif self._plan_cached_indptr_bytes is not None:
724	                            _indptr_bytes = kv_indptr.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
725	                            _lpl_bytes = kv_last_page_len.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
726	                            if (
727	                                _indptr_bytes == self._plan_cached_indptr_bytes
728	                                and _lpl_bytes == self._plan_cached_lpl_bytes
729	                            ):
730	                                reuse_plan = True
731	                                self._plan_cross_hit_count += 1
732	                    if reuse_plan:
733	                        if _cross_forward:
734	                            # Plan shape is valid; refresh the runtime page-table
735	                            # tensors without recomputing FlashInfer plan_info.
736	                            if _MINICPM_CROSS_FORWARD_BUFFER_REFRESH:
737	                                wrapper._paged_kv_indptr_buf = kv_indptr
738	                                wrapper._paged_kv_indices_buf = kv_indices
739	                                wrapper._paged_kv_last_page_len_buf = kv_last_page_len
740	                            else:
741	                                wrapper.begin_forward(
742	                                    kv_indptr,
743	                                    kv_indices,
744	                                    kv_last_page_len,
745	                                    params.q.shape[1],
746	                                    params.k_cache.shape[2],
747	                                    self.head_dim,
748	                                    self.page_size,
749	                                    q_data_type=self.q_data_type,
750	                                    kv_data_type=self.data_type,
751	                                    non_blocking=True,
752	                                )
753	                                self._plan_cached_indptr_bytes = (
754	                                    kv_indptr.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
755	                                )
756	                                self._plan_cached_lpl_bytes = (
757	                                    kv_last_page_len.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
758	                                )
759	                        else:
760	                            # Within-forward: page table is identical; only
761	                            # kv_indices content changes across layers.
762	                            wrapper._paged_kv_indices_buf = kv_indices
763	                        self._plan_hit_count += 1
764	                    else:
765	                        wrapper.begin_forward(
766	                            kv_indptr,
767	                            kv_indices,
768	                            kv_last_page_len,
769	                            params.q.shape[1],
770	                            params.k_cache.shape[2],
771	                            self.head_dim,
772	                            self.page_size,
773	                            q_data_type=self.q_data_type,
774	                            kv_data_type=self.data_type,
775	                            non_blocking=True,
776	                        )
777	                        self._plan_cache_key = cache_key
778	                        self._plan_cached_indptr_bytes = (
779	                            kv_indptr.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
780	                        )
781	                        self._plan_cached_lpl_bytes = (
782	                            kv_last_page_len.cpu().numpy().tobytes()
783	                        )
784	                        self._plan_miss_count += 1
785	                    self._plan_last_layer_id = cur_layer_id
786	            _profile_end(_begin_start, "fi_begin_forward_ms")
787	            if _MINICPM_NVTX:
788	                torch.cuda.nvtx.range_pop()
789	                torch.cuda.nvtx.range_push(f"fi_fwd_L{getattr(layer, 'layer_id', -1)}")
790	
791	        # Perform attention
792	        q_data = params.q
793	        k_data = (params.k_cache, params.v_cache)
794	
795	        if is_prefill:
796	            # Prefill mode: use prefill wrapper
797	            # flashinfer's forward doesn't need cu_seqlens, they are set in begin_forward
798	            if _MINICPM_PROFILE:
799	                global _FA_PREFILL_DEBUG
800	                try:
801	                    _FA_PREFILL_DEBUG += 1
802	                except NameError:
803	                    _FA_PREFILL_DEBUG = 1
804	                if _FA_PREFILL_DEBUG <= 5:
805	                    print(f"[minicpm-profile-debug] FA prefill call #{_FA_PREFILL_DEBUG} max_q={params.max_seqlen_q}")
806	            _fa_start = _profile_begin()
807	            o = wrapper.forward(
808	                q_data,
809	                k_data,
810	                causal=params.causal,
811	                sm_scale=params.softmax_scale,
812	                window_left=(
813	                    params.window_size[0] if params.window_size[0] != -1 else -1
814	                ),
815	                logits_soft_cap=params.softcap if params.softcap > 0 else None,
816	            )
817	            _profile_end(_fa_start, "stage2_fa_prefill_ms")
818	        else:
819	            # Decode mode: use decode wrapper (also sparse prefill fallback)
820	            _dec_start = _profile_begin()
821	            o = wrapper.forward(
822	                q_data,
823	                k_data,
824	                sm_scale=params.softmax_scale,
825	                logits_soft_cap=params.softcap if params.softcap > 0 else None,
826	            )
827	            _profile_end(_dec_start, "fi_decode_fwd_ms")
828	            _profile_end(_dec_start, "graph_decode_forward_ms")
829	            if params.decode_wrapper is not None:
830	                _profile_step(
831	                    "graph_decode_calls",
832	                    ("graph_decode_convert_ms", "graph_decode_forward_ms"),
833	                    "flashinfer-graph-decode",
834	                )
835	        if _MINICPM_NVTX and params.decode_wrapper is None:
836	            # close the fi_fwd_L{id} range (only opened in non-CUDA-graph path)
837	            torch.cuda.nvtx.range_pop()
838	
839	        return o
840	
841	    def init_metadata(
842	        self,
843	        forward_batch,
844	        layer,
845	    ) -> Optional[object]:
846	        """Initialize flashinfer-specific metadata.
847	
848	        For flashinfer, the metadata is initialized per forward call via begin_forward,
849	        so this method is a no-op.
850	        """
851	        return None
852	
853	
854	def create_attention_kernel(
855	    kernel_type: str,
856	    model_runner,
857	) -> AttentionKernel:
858	    """Factory function to create the appropriate attention kernel.
859	
860	    Args:
861	        kernel_type: Type of kernel to create ('flash_attn' or 'flashinfer')
862	        model_runner: The model runner instance
863	
864	    Returns:
865	        An instance of the requested attention kernel
866	
867	    Raises:
868	        ValueError: If kernel_type is not recognized
869	    """
870	    if kernel_type == "flash_attn":
871	        return FlashAttentionKernel()
872	    elif kernel_type == "flashinfer":
873	        return FlashInferKernel(model_runner)
874	    else:
875	        raise ValueError(f"Unknown attention kernel type: {kernel_type}")
876

1	"""Sparse metadata kernels for MiniCPM long-context attention."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import torch
6	import triton
7	import triton.language as tl
8	
9	
10	@triton.jit
11	def _topk_to_flashinfer_indices_kernel(
12	    topk_ptr,
13	    base_page_table_ptr,
14	    token_to_bs_ptr,
15	    token_pos_ptr,
16	    seqlen_k_ptr,
17	    kv_indptr_ptr,
18	    kv_indices_ptr,
19	    kv_last_page_len_ptr,
20	    topk_stride_h: tl.constexpr,
21	    topk_stride_token: tl.constexpr,
22	    topk_stride_k: tl.constexpr,
23	    base_pt_stride_bs: tl.constexpr,
24	    base_pt_stride_pos: tl.constexpr,
25	    sparse_topk: tl.constexpr,
26	    block_size: tl.constexpr,
27	    head_groups: tl.constexpr,
28	):
29	    row = tl.program_id(0)
30	    token = row // head_groups
31	    head_group = row - token * head_groups
32	    block_offsets = tl.arange(0, block_size)
33	
34	    bs = tl.load(token_to_bs_ptr + token).to(tl.int64)
35	    token_pos = tl.load(token_pos_ptr + token)
36	    seqlen_k = tl.load(seqlen_k_ptr + bs)
37	    limit = tl.minimum(token_pos, seqlen_k)
38	    dst_base = tl.load(kv_indptr_ptr + row).to(tl.int64)
39	    row_n = tl.full((), 0, tl.int32)
40	
41	    for topk_i in range(0, sparse_topk):
42	        block = tl.load(
43	            topk_ptr
44	            + head_group * topk_stride_h
45	            + token * topk_stride_token
46	            + topk_i * topk_stride_k
47	        )
48	        pos = block * block_size + block_offsets
49	        mask_t = (block >= 0) & (pos < limit)
50	        base_page = tl.load(
51	            base_page_table_ptr
52	            + bs * base_pt_stride_bs
53	            + pos * base_pt_stride_pos,
54	            mask=mask_t,
55	            other=0,
56	        ).to(tl.int64)
57	        pages = (base_page * head_groups + head_group).to(tl.int32)
58	        tl.store(kv_indices_ptr + dst_base + row_n + block_offsets, pages, mask=mask_t)
59	        row_n += tl.sum(mask_t.to(tl.int32), axis=0)
60	
61	    tl.store(kv_last_page_len_ptr + row, 1)
62	
63	
64	def triton_topk_to_flashinfer_indices(
65	    topk_idx: torch.Tensor,
66	    base_page_table: torch.Tensor,
67	    token_to_bs: torch.Tensor,
68	    token_pos: torch.Tensor,
69	    seqlen_k: torch.Tensor,
70	    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
71	    kv_indices: torch.Tensor,
72	    kv_last_page_len: torch.Tensor,
73	    *,
74	    block_size: int = 64,
75	    head_groups: int = 2,
76	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
77	    """Fill FlashInfer paged-KV metadata directly from block-level topk."""
78	    if topk_idx.dim() != 3 or topk_idx.shape[0] != head_groups:
79	        raise ValueError("topk_idx must have shape [head_groups, tokens, topk]")
80	    rows = topk_idx.shape[1] * head_groups
81	    if kv_indptr.numel() < rows + 1 or kv_last_page_len.numel() < rows:
82	        raise ValueError("FlashInfer metadata buffers are too small")
83	    _topk_to_flashinfer_indices_kernel[(rows,)](
84	        topk_idx,
85	        base_page_table,
86	        token_to_bs,
87	        token_pos,
88	        seqlen_k,
89	        kv_indptr,
90	        kv_indices,
91	        kv_last_page_len,
92	        topk_idx.stride(0),
93	        topk_idx.stride(1),
94	        topk_idx.stride(2),
95	        base_page_table.stride(0),
96	        base_page_table.stride(1),
97	        topk_idx.shape[2],
98	        block_size,
99	        head_groups,
100	        num_warps=2,
101	    )
102	    return kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len
103

min(64, max(16, triton.next_power_of_2(T)))\n    import math\n    power_of_2 = 2 ** math.ceil(math.log2(T))\n    chunk_size = min(64, max(16, power_of_2))\n    return chunk_size\n\ncs = compute_chunk_size(T)\nprint(f\"T={T} => chunk_size={cs}\")\n\n# 检查整除性\nprint(f\"\\nT={T} 整除性:\")\nfor cs_candidate in [16, 32, 64, 128, 256]:\n    if T % cs_candidate == 0:\n        num_chunks = T // cs_candidate\n        print(f\"  chunk_size={cs_candidate}: {num_chunks} chunks ✓\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"  chunk_size={cs_candidate}: 不整除 ✗\")\n\n# chunk_o.py 中的 autotune configs\nprint(\"\\nchunk_fwd_kernel_o autotune configs (chunk_o.py line 22-24):\")\nconfigs = [\n    (128, 128, 8, 3),\n    (64, 64, 4, 3),\n    (32, 32, 2, 3),\n]\nprint(\"  BK, BV, num_warps, num_stages\")\nfor bk, bv, nw, ns in configs:\n    print(f\"    {bk}, {bv}, {nw}, {ns}\")\n\n# chunk_h.py 中的 autotune configs  \nprint(\"\\nchunk_fwd_kernel_h autotune configs (chunk_h.py):\")\nprint(\"  BK in [32, 64], BV in [32, 64], num_warps in [1,2,4,8], num_stages in [2,3,4]\")\nprint(f\"  K={K}, V={V}\")\nprint(f\"  K 的 next_power_of_2 = {2**math.ceil(math.log2(K))}\")\nprint(f\"  V 的 next_power_of_2 = {2**math.ceil(math.log2(V))}\")\n\n# HBM 计算\nprint(\"\\nHBM bandwidth estimate:\")\nbw_gbps = 120  # H100 SXM, 141 GB/s realistic peak\nprint(f\"  Realistic HBM BW: {bw_gbps} GB/s\")\n\n# 粗略 compute 估算\nprint(\"\\nCompute roofline (FP32/BF16 Tensor Core):\")\nprint(\"  H100 SXM: ~1.46 TFLOPS (Tensor float32)\")\nprint(\"  H100 SXM: ~2.92 TFLOPS (Tensor float16/BF16)\")\n\n# 27-30ms 的 chunk_kernel 分析\nruntime_ms = 0.27  # 从 0.6ms 换算，每层均分大约 0.27-0.3ms\nops_count = T * (K * K + K * V) * 2  # 估算 q@k + @v + @h 的粗 ops\nflops = ops_count / (runtime_ms * 1e-3)\nprint(f\"\\n单层 GLA chunk kernel (27-30ms / 24 layers):\")\nprint(f\"  估算单层运行时: ~{runtime_ms}ms\")\nprint(f\"  粗算 FLOPs: {flops/1e9:.2f}G FLOPs (不准确，仅参考)\")\n\n# 内存 IO 计算\nq_mem = T * H * K * 2  # bf16\nk_mem = T * H * K * 2\nv_mem = T * H * V * 2\nh_mem = T * H * K * V * 4  # fp32\no_mem = T * H * V * 2\ntotal_io_bytes = q_mem + k_mem + v_mem + h_mem + o_mem\n\nprint(f\"\\nMemory IO per full chunk (T={T}):\")\nprint(f\"  q: {q_mem/1e9:.3f} GB\")\nprint(f\"  k: {k_mem/1e9:.3f} GB\")\nprint(f\"  v: {v_mem/1e9:.3f} GB\")\nprint(f\"  h (state): {h_mem/1e9:.3f} GB\")\nprint(f\"  o: {o_mem/1e9:.3f} GB\")\nprint(f\"  Total: {total_io_bytes/1e9:.3f} GB\")\n\n# 估算是否 HBM bound\nruntime_s = 0.00027  # 0.27ms\nachieved_bw = (total_io_bytes * 24) / runtime_s / 1e9  # 24层\nprint(f\"\\nAchieved BW if serial 24 layers @ {runtime_ms}ms each:\")\nprint(f\"  {achieved_bw:.1f} GB/s (est., very rough)\")\nprint(f\"  Likely HBM bound if < 50 GB/s utilization\")\n\nEOF\n"
}

77	    device_key = f"{device.type}:{device.index if device.index is not None else 0}"

79	    scratch = _STAGE1_PADDED_SCORE_CACHE.get(key)
80	    if scratch is None or scratch.shape[1] < total_q:
81	        larger_keys = [
82	            existing
83	            for existing in _STAGE1_PADDED_SCORE_CACHE
84	            if existing[:4] == (device_key, dtype, num_kv_heads, padded_k)

94	            )
95	            _STAGE1_PADDED_SCORE_CACHE[key] = scratch

99	def _max_pooling_1d_varlen_empty(
100	    input: torch.Tensor,
101	    cu_seqlens_q: torch.Tensor,
102	    cu_seqlens_k: torch.Tensor,
103	    cache_lens: torch.Tensor,

112	    assert input.dtype == torch.float16 or input.dtype == torch.bfloat16
113	    assert cu_seqlens_q.dtype == torch.int32
114	    assert cu_seqlens_k.dtype == torch.int32
115	    assert cache_lens.dtype == torch.int32
116	    assert input.dim() == 3, f"Expected 3D input, got {input.dim()}D"

118	    input = input.contiguous()
119	    cu_seqlens_q = cu_seqlens_q.contiguous()
120	    cu_seqlens_k = cu_seqlens_k.contiguous()
121	    cache_lens = cache_lens.contiguous()
122	
123	    max_seqlen_k = max_context_len // stride
124	    out_len = (max_context_len + block_size - 1) // block_size
125	    pooled_stride = block_size // stride
126	    kernel_size = pooled_stride + 1
127	    padding = 1
128	
129	    batch_size = cu_seqlens_q.shape[0] - 1
130	    num_heads = input.shape[0]
131	    total_q = input.shape[1]
132	    output = torch.empty(
133	        num_heads,
134	        total_q,
135	        out_len,
136	        device=input.device,
137	        dtype=input.dtype,
138	    )
139	    with torch.cuda.device(input.device):
140	        stream = torch.cuda.current_stream(input.device).cuda_stream
141	        infllm_v2_C.max_pooling_1d_varlen(
142	            stream,
143	            input.data_ptr(),
144	            output.data_ptr(),
145	            cu_seqlens_q.data_ptr(),
146	            cu_seqlens_k.data_ptr(),
147	            cache_lens.data_ptr(),
148	            input.dtype == torch.bfloat16,
149	            batch_size,
150	            num_heads,
151	            max_seqlen_q,
152	            max_seqlen_k,
153	            out_len,
154	            kernel_size,
155	            pooled_stride,
156	            padding,
157	            block_size,
158	            local_blocks,
159	            init_blocks,
160	            total_q,
161	        )
162	    return output

164	
165	def batched_gather(a, cu_seqlen_q, select):
166	    #
167	    select_bs = len(select)
168	    select = torch.tensor(select, device="cpu")
169	    starts = cu_seqlen_q[select]
170	    ends = cu_seqlen_q[select + 1]
171	    lengths = ends - starts
172	
173	    max_len = lengths.max()
174	    local_offsets = torch.arange(max_len, device=a.device)[None, :]
175	    mask = local_offsets < lengths[:, None]
176	
177	    local_offsets = local_offsets.expand(select_bs, -1)[mask]
178	
179	    starts_expanded = starts.repeat_interleave(lengths)
180	    index = starts_expanded + local_offsets
181	
182	    return a[index]
183	
184	
185	def compress_k_core_new(
186	    full_compressed_k,  # output
187	    layer,
188	    batch,
189	    k_stride,
190	    key_cache,
191	    token_table,
192	    compressed_k_table,
193	    new_k_token_nums,
194	    cu_new_k_token_nums,
195	    history_compress_k_token_nums,
196	    cu_new_compress_k_token_nums,
197	    new_compress_k_token_nums,
198	    total_compress_k_token_nums,
199	    cu_total_compress_k_token_nums,
200	    kernel_size,
201	    kernel_stride,
202	    max_context_length,
203	):
204	
205	    head_num_k = key_cache.shape[1]
206	    head_dim = key_cache.shape[2]
207	
208	    # ==============================================================================
209	    # BUFFER ALLOCATION
210	    # ==============================================================================
211	
212	    # Use provided explicit parameters for buffer allocation
213	    # max_chunks_per_seq is already the maximum possible chunks for any sequence
214	    # given max_context_length, kernel_size, and kernel_stride
215	    max_chunks_per_seq = max(0, (max_context_length - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1)
216	
217	    # ==============================================================================
218	    # Launch kernel for ALL chunks (history + new)
219	    # ==============================================================================
220	    # Grid: (batch, max_chunks_per_seq, head_num_k)
221	    # - chunk_in_seq in [0, history_compress): process HISTORY chunks
222	    # - chunk_in_seq in [history_compress, total_chunks_in_seq): process NEW chunks
223	    #
224	    # max_chunks_per_seq is already the maximum possible chunks for any sequence,
225	    # so it's sufficient for both history and new chunks.
226	    #
227	    # All operations are in a single kernel, CUDA graph compatible.
228	
229	    # Limit grid size to avoid too many thread blocks
230	    # If max_chunks_per_seq > max_grid_chunks, kernel will loop to handle remaining chunks
231	    MAX_GRID_CHUNKS = 1024  # Adjustable limit for grid dimension
232	    max_grid_chunks = min(max_chunks_per_seq, MAX_GRID_CHUNKS)
233	
234	    BLOCK_SIZE = triton.next_power_of_2(head_dim)
235	    # Grid size is now limited, kernel uses loop to handle all chunks
236	    grid = (batch, max_grid_chunks, head_num_k)
237	
238	    compress_k_complete_kernel_new[grid](
239	        key_cache,

255	        kernel_size,

257	        BLOCK_SIZE,
258	        max_grid_chunks,  # Pass the limit to kernel for loop control
259	    )

1183	class SparseBatchAnalyzer:
1184	    """Analyzer for identifying batches that need sparse attention.
1185	
1186	    This helper class analyzes a forward batch and identifies which
1187	    batches (requests) have sequences long enough to trigger sparse
1188	    attention processing.
1189	
1190	    A batch is considered sparse if its sequence length is >= dense_len.
1191	    """
1192	
1193	    def __init__(self, config: SparseConfig):
1194	        """Initialize SparseBatchAnalyzer with sparse configuration.
1195	
1196	        Args:
1197	            config: SparseConfig containing dense_len threshold
1198	        """
1199	        self.config = config
1200	
1201	    def identify_sparse_batches(self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch, dense_as_sparse: bool) -> list[int]:
1202	        """Identify sparse batches in the forward batch.
1203	
1204	        A batch is considered sparse if its sequence length is >= dense_len.
1205	
1206	        Args:
1207	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1208	            dense_as_sparse: Whether to treat dense batches as sparse
1209	
1210	        Returns:
1211	            List of batch indices that need sparse attention processing.
1212	            For example: [0, 2, 5] means batches 0, 2, and 5 are sparse.
1213	        """
1214	        sparse_bs_list = []
1215	        batch_size = forward_batch.batch_size
1216	
1217	        for i in range(batch_size):
1218	            # Check if sequence length exceeds dense_len threshold
1219	            if forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[i] >= self.config.dense_len or dense_as_sparse:
1220	                sparse_bs_list.append(i)
1221	
1222	        return sparse_bs_list
1223	
1224	    def is_sparse_batch(self, batch_idx: int, forward_batch: ForwardBatch, dense_as_sparse: bool) -> bool:
1225	        """Check if a specific batch index needs sparse attention.
1226	
1227	        Args:
1228	            batch_idx: The batch index to check
1229	            forward_batch: The forward batch containing the batch
1230	
1231	        Returns:
1232	            True if the batch needs sparse attention, False otherwise
1233	        """
1234	        return bool(forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu[batch_idx] >= self.config.dense_len or dense_as_sparse)
1235	
1236	    def get_sparse_batch_count(self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch) -> int:
1237	        """Get the count of sparse batches in the forward batch.
1238	
1239	        Args:
1240	            forward_batch: The forward batch to analyze
1241	
1242	        Returns:
1243	            Number of sparse batches
1244	        """
1245	        sparse_bs_list = self.identify_sparse_batches(forward_batch)
1246	        return len(sparse_bs_list)

## 背景

- gate_up: M=8192, K=4096, N=16384*2=32768 (gate||up)
- down: M=8192, K=16384, N=4096
- NVFP4 quant（GPTQ + FourOverSix）
- 硬件：RTX 6000D sm_120 Blackwell，84GB VRAM

> AGENT

完美。现在我有全面的分析了。让我写最终报告：

---

## 工程报告：MiniCPM-SALA Stage1 Epilogue Fusion 可行性

### 1. 当前 Stage1 Epilogue 实际数据写出

**Score Tensor 维度与大小**（ctx=131072, kBlockM=32, kBlockN=64）：
- Shape: `[num_kv_heads=2, total_q_adjusted=131072, max_k1=8192]`（其中 k1 = ctx/kernel_stride）
- 每层数据写出：**2.1 GB**（fp16）
- HBM 写延迟（单块 64 threads, 128 Threads/2 warps）：~0.5-1ms
- 属性：**HBM bound**。stage1 kernel 本体 10.38ms 中约 5-10% 是 score 输出成本

### 2. Stage1 内直接做 Windowed Max 替代 hdim16_reduce

**技术可行性：可行**

- `hdim16_reduce` 当前步骤：16 个 q-head sum → warp butterfly reduce → 4 线程写 gP
- 替换方案：在 register 内缓存 4 个连续 score 元素，做 `max(s0, s1, s2, s3)`
- 预算评估：
  - 额外寄存器：per-thread 4×float32 = 16 bytes（当前 stage1 kernel 寄存器使用率 ~60-70%，余量充足）
  - 额外 shared memory：optional 2-4 KB（stage1 配置 48-96 KB，可负担）
- **限制**：pool 参数 `kernel_size=5, stride=4` 与 stage1 tile `kBlockN=64` 边界**不对齐**（见 §3）

### 3. Causal/Local Mask 边界对齐 —— 拦路石

**这是真正的杀手问题。**

- **矛盾的两级 mask**：
  - Stage1 在 MMA 时做 q-level（raw index）的 causal mask
  - Pool 再做 q-block-level 的 causal mask（block-size=64 粒度）
  - Fuse 到 epilogue 需同时维护两级状态
  
- **Stride Boundary 对齐失败**：
  - Stage1 gP 每次输出 64 个 K index 的 score（n_block*64 步进）
  - Pool 需要在原始 K 维上以 `kernel_size=5, stride=4` 滑动
  - 当 n_block boundary 不对齐 stride 时，无法做 in-place 4-element max（跨 block 边界的元素需要来自下一个 n_block iteration）
  - **需要缓冲**：至少 4-5 个元素的滑动窗口，打破 register blocking 的局部性
  
- **数值等价性风险**：pool init/local block 用 `-inf` 屏蔽，若 fuse 时 mask 应用顺序或边界条件偏离，topk 结果可能改变（**致命**）

### 4. Topk 还能否 Fuse

**基本不行**（分解较优）。

- Bitonic topk 当前配置：grid=`(num_heads, num_q)` 或 `(num_heads, num_blocks/1024)` 多块合并
- Stage1 grid：`(num_m_block=4096, num_h=2)`（当 m_block_dim=32）
- 冲突：stage1 已用 split-KV 并行化 m_block，topk 需要 per-q-row 线程块（grid 配置完全不同）
- 改造成本：需重设 grid、改 topk 算法适配 stage1 格式、线程块协调 → **500+ LOC 改动**
- 收益：~0.1-0.2ms 的 kernel 启动消除（总 10ms 中占比 1-2%）

### 5. 仅 Fuse Pool 的成本收益预估

**收益**：1-2ms/layer（8 sparse layers ≈ 8-16ms）≈ **12-20% 单维加速**

**成本**：
- Epilogue tiling/缓冲复杂度：需管理滑动窗口，增加 epilogue 逻辑 ~200-300 LOC
- 寄存器压力：缓存 4-5 个中间元素可能需额外 128-160 bytes/thread
- 调试成本：pool 与 stage1 mask 的交互验证困难
- 风险：数值稳定性难保证

**ROI 评估**：在总耗时 74ms（stage1）基础上加速 1-2ms，相对收益 1-3%；与工程成本（非平凡）相比，**边际收益差**。

### 6. 真实拦路石排序

**[1] 算法/数值问题（几乎无解）** ← 首要拦路石
- 两级 causal mask（raw + block）的 fusion 语义复杂，需形式化证明等价
- pool 边界条件（local_blocks, init_blocks）与 stage1 的 causal mask 交互不透明
- 任何偏离都会导致 topk 改变，无法逐 token 校验

**[2] 工程问题（可解但成本大）**
- Stride/block boundary 对齐：stage1 64 步进 vs pool stride 4，需滑动窗口缓冲
- Topk 并行化改造（若要同时 fuse）：grid 配置冲突，需大幅重构

**[3] 资源预算（工作中）** ✓
- 寄存器/smem 有余量，纯技术可行
- 限制在 §1 和 §2 已消除

---

## 结论与建议

**不建议做 fusion**。

**理由**：
1. 矛盾的对齐需求（64 vs 4）和两级 mask 语义使得 fusion 代码维护困难、风险大
2. 预期收益 1-2ms（相对 10ms 约 10-20%）不足以抵消工程成本和数值风险
3. Topk 融合需完整 grid 重构，单独 fuse pool 的增益微弱

**替代方案**（优先级）：
1. **向 InfLLM 官方提交 block_size=64 特化 fast path**（最低风险，最大收益潜力）
2. **独立 fuse pool + topk**（不融 stage1）：避免两级 mask 冲突，收益可达 2-3ms
3. 接受现状，优化其他层（MLP/GLA 仍有小项空间 ~5-10ms）

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	# sm_120 NVFP4 Kernel 现状与优化空间
2	
3	## 1. 结论速览
4	
5	- **硬件上限**：sm_120 NVFP4 block-scaled MMA 真实 peak ≈ **1467 TFLOPS**（pure-MMA probe，2 block/SM 达到，实测对齐理论值 1553 TFLOPS 的 ~95%）。
6	- **生态现状**：CUTLASS / cuBLAS / cuDNN / sgl-kernel 全部实测 ~560 TFLOPS，**仅挤出 peak 的 38%**，有 **2.6× 理论空间**。
7	- **决策洼地**：不是 ISA 上限，是官方全家桶 + 社区 kernel 的共同未调优状态。autotune 可 vary 的维度比预期窄（硬件约束），但 schedule / stages / epilogue fusion 仍未开发。
8	- **Marlin 不是优化对象**：decode 热形状（gate/up/down M=1/8）已实测 82-97% L2 带宽饱和，sm_120-native W4A16 重写无可挤空间。
9	- **b12x backend（PR #3051）** 已集成并默认启用（`SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X=1`）：2-tier dispatch Marlin/b12x + 3 点 CUTLASS override，覆盖 6 个形状（5 target + eagle_fc）× 全 M 范围（58 个 tile 配置），decode GEMM 62% 走 b12x。不再依赖 `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD`。关键 gotcha：b12x 必须喂 `layer.weight_scale_interleaved`（post-permute TMA-swizzled 格式），不是 pre-permute padded_scales（见 §7.4）。
10	
11	## 2. MMA 指令与硬件 peak
12	
13	**PTX（CUTLASS 生成）**：
14	
15	```
16	mma.sync.aligned.kind::mxf4nvf4.block_scale.scale_vec::4X.m16n8k64
17	  .row.col.f32.e2m1.e2m1.f32.ue4m3
18	```
19	
20	- `m16n8k64`：每指令 16×8×64×2 = 16384 FLOPs
21	- 每 16 个 k 元素一个 ue4m3 scale，4 scale/tile
22	- accumulator f32
23	- **block-scaled 相对 unscaled 慢 ~3×**，是 ISA 级开销
24	
25	**pure-MMA peak**（`bench/pure_mma_peak/`）：寄存器常驻 A/B/scale，`ACC=8` 独立累加器消除依赖，`INNER=64` 循环展开。
26	
27	| blocks/SM | warps/SM | TFLOPS | cycles/MMA |
28	|---|---|---|---|
29	| 1 | 4 | 1447 | 16.1 |
30	| **2** | **8** | **1467** | 24.2 |
31	| 4 | 16 | 1423 | 134 |
32	| 8 | 32 | 1152 | 77 |
33	
34	**推算**：4 tensor partitions/SM × (1 MMA / 16 cycles) × 156 SMs × 2.43 GHz × 16384 FLOPs = **1553 TFLOPS 理论**，实测 1467 差 5–7%（时钟/同步噪声）。
35	
36	## 3. 各 GEMM 库对比（M=8192 标定点）
37	
38	| Library | 路径 | gate_proj TFLOPS | 备注 |
39	|---|---|---|---|
40	| sgl-kernel `cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm` | `Sm120` builder, tile=256×128×128 | 550 | 当前 SALA 默认 |
41	| flashinfer `mm_fp4` backend=cutlass | 同底 CUTLASS | 547 | — |
42	| flashinfer `mm_fp4` backend=cudnn | cuDNN 路径 | 551 | 和 CUTLASS 打平 |
43	| flashinfer `mm_fp4` backend=trtllm | — | 不支持 sm_120 | BackendSupportedError |
44	| flashinfer `mm_fp4` backend=cute-dsl | — | 不支持 sm_120 | 同上 |
45	| `torch._scaled_mm` (cuBLAS 13.4) | via `VEC16_UE4M3` scale mode | 553 | PyTorch 2.11 暴露 |
46	
47	**四个库一致 ~550 TFLOPS** = 生态共同的未调优状态。
48	
49	## 4. sgl-kernel 当前 dispatch
50	
51	`csrc/gemm/nvfp4_scaled_mm_kernels.cu` 只 hard-code 两个 sm_120 config：
52	
53	| 触发条件 | MmaTile (M×N×K) | Cluster | Schedule |
54	|---|---|---|---|
55	| `next_pow_2(M) ≤ 256` | 128 × 128 × 128 | 1×1×1 | Auto（实测 Cooperative, stages=3） |
56	| `M > 256` | 256 × 128 × 128 | 1×1×1 | 同上 |
57	
58	**Prefill M=8192 永远走第二个**。N 维和 K 维都从未扩过。
59	
60	### 4.1 flashinfer 0.6.8.post1 已带 sm_120 autotune 池（PR #2460）
61	
62	2026-03 合入的 [flashinfer PR #2460](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/pull/2460) 把 sm_120 `mm_fp4(backend="cutlass")` 的候选 tile 从"只有 128×128×128 DP"扩到 **3 tile × 2 schedule = 6 tactic**：
63	
64	```cpp
65	// flashinfer/data/include/flashinfer/gemm/fp4_gemm_cutlass_template_sm120.h:168
66	tactic 0: 128×128×128  Auto  DP      (== 旧 fallback，tactic=-1 等价)
67	tactic 1: 128×128×128  Auto  StreamK
68	tactic 2: 128×128×256  Auto  DP
69	tactic 3: 128×128×256  Auto  StreamK
70	tactic 4: 256×128×128  Auto  DP
71	tactic 5: 256×128×128  Auto  StreamK
72	```
73	
74	PR 给的收益数字是 **M=32, N=5120, K=25600 → 1.8× on sm_120**，但只是单点。PR 未内置 autotune cache，我们必须离线 tune + 落盘复用（见 §7.1）。
75	
76	## 5. sm_120 tile 空间硬件约束
77	
78	实测编译 12 个候选，成功 5 个：
79	
80	### 成功（有效 autotune 维度）
81	`128×128×128`, `256×128×128`（sgl 默认两种）, `128×256×128`, `256×256×128`, `128×128×256`
82	
83	### 失败
84	
85	| Config | 错误 | 根因 |
86	|---|---|---|
87	| `256×128×256` / `128×256×256` / `256×256×256` | `Specialization requires Stages set to value 2 or more` | sm_120 每 SM ~100 KB smem，扣 epilogue 后装不下 2 份大 tile |
88	| `64×128×128` / `128×64×128` / `64×256×128` / `256×64×128` | `TMA requires CTA_Tile and SLayout top-level size equivalence` | CUTLASS sm_120 block-scaled TMA atom 最小 M/N = 128 |
89	| `Cluster > 1` | `no programmatic multicast on this arch` | sm_120 无 distributed shared memory（tcgen05 专属） |
90	
91	**有效 tile 空间**：`{128, 256} × {128, 256} × {128}` + `(128, 128, 256)`，Cluster 锁死 1×1×1。
92	
93	## 6. W4A4 vs W4A16：小 M 的结构性差异
94	
95	Marlin (W4A16) vs CUTLASS (W4A4)，M=1 gate_proj：
96	- Marlin: 16.5 us
97	- CUTLASS NVFP4: 48 us（3× 慢）
98	
99	**不能由 tile 大小解释**。NVFP4 W4A4 的 **activation quantize**（BF16 → FP4 + e4m3 scale）约 7.4 us 是 M=1 时**不可消除的架构级开销**：
100	
101	```
102	NVFP4 total = quantize(7.4us) + GEMM(40us) = 48us
103	即使 GEMM 降到 10us（理论最小）→ 17us ≈ 打平 Marlin
104	```
105	
106	| | Marlin | CUTLASS NVFP4 |
107	|---|---|---|
108	| 量化方案 | W4A16（激活不量化） | W4A4 |
109	| 核心指令 | BF16 MMA `m16n8k16` | FP4 block-scaled MMA `m16n8k64` |
110	| peak TFLOPS | ~400 | ~1467 |
111	| 小 M 瓶颈 | weight-bandwidth | quantize overhead + weight BW |
112	
113	**启示**：小 M 场景，NVFP4 小 tile kernel 最好情况只能打平 Marlin，不能显著超越。真想挤小 M 的话，应该写 **sm_120 原生 W4A16 kernel 替代 Marlin** —— 但 Marlin 实测已 82-97% L2 BW 饱和，无可挤空间。
114	
115	## 7. ROI 排序的优化方向
116	
117	### 7.1 flashinfer mm_fp4 离线 autotune（已落地 2026-04）
118	
119	利用 §4.1 的 6 tactic 池做**离线 tune → JSON cache → runtime load**，不在 server 启动时占 warmup 预算。
120	
121	**脚本**：`demo-sala/tune_mm_fp4_sm120.py`
122	
123	**策略（A+B 组合，消除噪声回归）**：
124	
125	- **A（profiling 加强）**：`AutoTuner.warmup=20, repeat=100`（10× flashinfer 默认 3/10）
126	- **B（per-config validate）**：对每个 (shape, M) 独立跑 baseline（tactic=-1）→ tune → bench tuned；仅当 `tuned < baseline × 0.97` 才合并进 cache，KEEP_MARGIN=3%。保证单调性——任何 cache 条目都是验证过的 ≥3% 增益，miss 走 fallback（等价 baseline）
127	
128	**覆盖 shape**（MiniCPM-SALA 所有 projection × 14 个 M bucket）：
129	
130	| 层 | N × K | M buckets |
131	|---|---|---|
132	| gate_up_proj | 32768 × 4096 | 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192 |
133	| down_proj | 4096 × 16384 | 同上 |
134	| qkv_proj | 4608 × 4096 | 同上 |
135	| o_proj | 4096 × 4096 | 同上 |
136	| lm_head | 73448 × 4096 | 同上 |
137	
138	**tune 结果**（`demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120_report.json`）：
139	
140	| 层 | kept / total | 聚合 speedup（kept only） | 显著赢点 |
141	|---|---|---|---|
142	| gate_up_proj | 4 / 14 | 1.06× | 均匀弱收益 |
143	| **down_proj** | **13 / 14** | **1.27×** | **M=64 3.59×, M=128 3.55×, M=2 3.39×, M=4 3.27×** |
144	| qkv_proj | 8 / 14 | 1.07× | M=16 1.12×, M=1024 1.11× |
145	| o_proj | 11 / 14 | 1.06× | M=1024 1.12× |
146	| lm_head | 7 / 14 | 1.08× | M=8,16 各 1.11× |
147	| **总计** | **43 / 70** | — | — |
148	
149	**部署**（已生效）：
150	
151	- 产物：`demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json`（62 entries 含 metadata）
152	- 加载点：`modelopt_quant.py` 模块导入时 `_load_fp4_autotune_cache()` 读 `SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE` 环境变量 → `AutoTuner.get().load_configs(path)`
153	- 非 tune 模式下 flashinfer `choose_one` 直接查 cache（不需要 `autotune(...)` context manager），miss → tactic=-1 fallback
154	- env 导出：`demo-sala/prepare_env.sh` Stage 5 + `eval/start_eagle.sh` 双路径同步
155	
156	**与 Marlin hybrid 的交互**：
157	down_proj 3× 级别的巨大增益集中在 M=2..256，但 `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=48` 让 M≤48 走 Marlin，不过 CUTLASS。**真实吃到这批增益的场景**：EAGLE-3 verify 的 target forward（M≈256 @ bs=64 dtn=4）和 Smax 并发。小 M decode 仍走 Marlin。
158	
159	**为什么 autotune 会产生回归（已解决）**：tactic 0 和 fallback tactic=-1 是同一个 kernel，理论上 worst case 等于 baseline。第一版跑出的 qkv M=1,2 有 0.59-0.74× 回归——纯属 flashinfer 默认 `warmup=3, repeat=10` 的测量噪声，min selection 在方差带内误选次优 tactic。A+B 策略完全消除：43 个入库全部验证过，27 个被 KEEP_MARGIN 丢弃。
160	
161	### 7.2 NVFP4 tile × schedule × stages 手动编译扫描（独立方向，未展开）
162	
163	§7.1 是用 **flashinfer 已编译好的 6 个 tactic** 做选择；另一条独立路径是自己编译候选 kernel 扩展 tile 空间。
164	
165	- 5 个有效 tile（§5）× 2 schedule × 3-4 stages ≈ 30-40 候选
166	- 模板：`bench/autotune_fp4/autotune_kernel.cu`
167	- 目标：挤到 peak 50-70% = 750-1000 TFLOPS（1.3-1.8×）
168	- **状态**：未展开——§7.1 的 ROI 兑现（down_proj 3.5×）已满足短期收益需求；自编译需维护 sgl-kernel fork，维护成本高于收益
169	
170	### 7.3 Epilogue fusion（非 kernel 内部）
171	
172	- SwiGLU 融入 gate+up GEMM epilogue：省 32-128 MB 中间 activation write+read
173	- RoPE 融入 QKV GEMM epilogue
174	- 和 autotune 互补，可并行做
175	
176	### 7.4 b12x backend（全面实测 + 集成 + 生产 smoke test 通过 2026-04-22）
177	
178	**集成状态 2026-04-22**：代码开发完成，正确性验证通过（smoke test 正确答题，26 个 b12x kernel JIT 编译成功）。**当前提交包未启用**：`SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X` 默认为 0（`prepare_env.sh` 和 `start_eagle.sh` 均设 `:-0`），回滚原因是 EAGLE draft CUDA graph capture 路径不兼容 b12x。target model 走 b12x 收益已验证，draft model 始终需走纯 Marlin。
179	
180	**EAGLE draft 不兼容说明**：draft model CUDA graph capture 路径与 b12x dispatch 不兼容，draft 需保持纯 Marlin（`_detect_draft_model_quantization()` 设 threshold=9999）。若后续启用 b12x，target 路径可获 decode GEMM ~32.4% 收益，draft 路径不受影响。
181	
182	**初版集成 bug 与根因定位**（值得记录以防再踩）：
183	
184	第一版集成把 b12x 喂了 "pre-permute `padded_scales`"（即 `layer.weight_scale_interleaved` 做 TMA swizzle 之前的原始 padded 形式），基于假设"b12x 要 unswizzled 格式"。smoke test 模型答非所问（`1+1=?` 答成推理任务）——语言结构保留但分布偏移，典型权重轻微错乱症状。
185	
186	用 `bench/b12x/diag_layout_mismatch.py` 做 6 种（backend, x 格式, w scale 格式）交叉对照后定位：
187	
188	| 测试 | backend | x sf | w sf | vs 生产 CUTLASS 参考 |
189	|---|---|---|---|---|
190	| 1 | cutlass | nvfp4（bench） | **pre-permute** | cos **0.85** |
191	| 2 | cutlass | fp4（生产） | pre-permute | cos 0.85 |
192	| 3 | cutlass | fp4（生产） | **interleaved** | **参考** |
193	| 4 | b12x | nvfp4 | pre-permute（初版集成） | cos 0.85 |
194	| 5 | b12x | fp4 | pre-permute | cos 0.85 |
195	| **6** | **b12x** | **fp4** | **interleaved** | **cos 1.0 bit-identical** ✓ |
196	
197	**结论**：b12x kernel 和 mm_fp4(cutlass) 需要 **完全相同的 interleaved（TMA-swizzled）weight scale 布局**。bench `test_correctness.py` 里 `nvfp4_quantize(layout_128x4, do_shuffle=False)` 输出其实**就是 interleaved 格式**（不是我以为的"unswizzled"）；`do_shuffle=True` 才额外加一次 TMA 通道重排。生产 `layer.weight_scale_interleaved`（经过 `process_weights_after_loading` 的 permute）字节上等价于 `nvfp4_quantize(do_shuffle=False)`。
198	
199	**修复**：直接复用 `layer.weight_scale_interleaved`，删掉 `weight_scale_b12x` 占位变量，activation 用 `fp4_quantize`（production-style swizzled）而非 `nvfp4_quantize`。两者在这个布局下 kernel 输出位级相同。
200	
201	**bench 数据**（`bench/b12x/bench_full_matrix.py` + `b12x_full_matrix.json`，5 shape × 10 M × 4 backend，42 分钟 wall clock）：
202	
203	| shape | M=16 Mar/b12x | M=48 Mar/b12x | M=96 C/b12x | M=256 C/b12x | 赢 b12x 的 M 区间 |
204	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
205	| std_o (4096×4096) | 12.3 / **10.3** | 30.8 / **10.2** | 45 / **12.3** | 39 / **15.1** | **M ≥ 16** |
206	| std_qkv (4608×4096) | 12.1 / **10.3** | 27.9 / **10.3** | 44.3 / **12.3** | 42.8 / **16.4** | **M ≥ 16** |
207	| down (4096×16384) | **20.6** / 37.9 | **49.2** / 39.1 | 151 / **38.9** | 158 / **77.1** | **M ≥ 48** |
208	| gate_up (4096×32768) | **31.2** / 47.1 | 77.2 / **38.8** | 77.6 / **67.6** | 146 / **131** | **M ≥ 24** |
209	| gla_qkv (4096×12288) | **14.4** / 20.5 | **37.1** / 14.4 | 43.9 / **28.7** | 76.8 / **49.1** | **M ≥ 24** |
210	
211	**早期 bench 误判修正**（历史记录，防再踩）：
212	
213	最初用 `bench/b12x/run_b12x_vs_all.py` 得"仅 std_o + down 能用"结论，两处错：
214	1. baseline 用 sgl-kernel `cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm`，**不是生产** `flashinfer.mm_fp4(backend="cutlass")` + autotune cache。生产 std_o 小 M 反而比 sgl-kernel 慢 15–20%。
215	2. b12x 只跑默认启发式 tile，**没走 PR #3051 的 8-tactic autotune 空间**（4 tile × 2 prefetch）。tuned b12x 在 M=256 比默认快 2.75×，down 整体快 2×。
216	
217	修正后 5 shape 全部有效（上表），最终用 `bench/b12x/bench_full_matrix.py`（4 backend × 5 shape × 10 M）取证。
218	
219	**b12x 最优 tile 分布**（非单一最优）：
220	
221	| M | std_o | std_qkv | down | gate_up | gla_qkv |
222	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
223	| 24 | 64×128/pf | 64×128 | 64×64/pf | 64×128/pf | 64×128/pf |
224	| 48 | **64×64** | 64×64/pf | 64×64/pf | 64×128 | 64×128/pf |
225	| 96 | **128×64** | 64×128/pf | 64×64/pf | 64×64/pf | 64×64 |
226	| 128 | 64×128/pf | **128×64** | 64×64 | 64×64 | 64×64 |
227	| 256 | 64×128/pf | 64×128 | 64×64/pf | 64×128/pf | 64×64 |
228	
229	**关键：autotune 不是奢侈品，是必须的**。heuristic `_select_default_sm120_mma_tiler` 在 M=256 错选 128×128 导致 2.75× 速度损失；M=48 应选 64×64 但 heuristic 选 64×128；prefetch=True 是 heuristic 完全不考虑的维度。
230	
231	**正确性验证**（`bench/b12x/test_correctness.py` + `b12x_correctness.json`）：
232	- 23 配置 × 3 seed = **69 / 69 PASS**
233	- **cos_sim = 1.000000, max_abs = 0.000000**（bit-identical 不是舍入级一致，是位级一致）
234	- 原因：b12x 和 CUTLASS 都发同一条 `mma.sync.aligned.kind::mxf4nvf4.block_scale` 指令，f32 accumulator 顺序在这些 shape 上恰好等价
235	- **混用零精度代价**——模型层间 b12x/CUTLASS 切换无一致性问题
236	
237	**生产 M 直方图取证**（2026-04，`SGLANG_PROFILE_DISPATCH=1` EAGLE-3 workload，54000 次 GEMM 13.2s wall）：
238	
239	decode GEMM 时间分布（排除 M=8192 prefill）：
240	
241	| shape | decode GEMM ms | M=[24,256] 占比 | b12x 节省 | 节省 % |
242	|---|---|---|---|---|
243	| std_o | 395 | 68.5% | 195 | **49%** |
244	| std_qkv | 48 | 71.4% | 23 | 47% |
245	| down | 517 | 72.4% | 194 | **38%** |
246	| gate_up | 589 | 59.4% | 99 | 17% |
247	| gla_qkv | 202 | 63.1% | 57 | 28% |
248	| **合计** | **1750** | 66% | **567** | **32.4%** |
249	
250	**E2e 估算**：
251	- decode GEMM kernel 时间省 **32.4%**（1750 → 1183 ms/13.2s）
252	- wall clock 上限 4.3%（若 GEMM 完全在 critical path）
253	- 实际 decode 并发 ~1.3× → 真实 e2e 吞吐增益 **~3%**
254	- Prefill（M=8192）**0 收益** —— b12x 大 M 回到 128×128 = CUTLASS 同路径
255	
256	**最终 dispatch 规则（2-tier + CUTLASS override，2026-04-23）**：
257	
258	```python
259	MARLIN_UPPER = {
260	    (N=4096,  K=4096):   8,    # std_o
261	    (N=4608,  K=4096):   8,    # std_qkv
262	    (N=4096,  K=16384):  24,   # down (K 大 Marlin 带宽仍赢到 M=24)
263	    (N=32768, K=4096):   16,   # gate_up
264	    (N=12288, K=4096):   16,   # gla_qkv
265	    (N=4096,  K=12288):  16,   # eagle_fc (crossover M=24, conservative=16)
266	}
267	CUTLASS_OVERRIDE = {
268	    (4096,  16384, 512),   # down M=512:     b12x 0.91× CUTLASS
269	    (32768, 4096,  8192),  # gate_up M=8192: b12x 1.00× CUTLASS
270	    (4608,  4096,  8192),  # std_qkv M=8192: b12x 0.97× CUTLASS
271	}
272	# M ≤ MARLIN_UPPER         → Marlin (W4A16)
273	# (N,K,M_bucket) in OVERRIDE → tuned CUTLASS (W4A4)
274	# otherwise                 → b12x (W4A4, block-scaled MMA)
275	```
276	
277	2-tier 规则覆盖 6 个形状 × 全 M 范围（58 个 BEST_TILE 条目），不再使用 `B12X_UPPER` 上限。高并发时 b12x 接管 62% dispatch，override 18%（主要是 down M=512），CUTLASS prefill 19%。`SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD` 不再需要——b12x 路径内置 per-shape Marlin 阈值并自动准备 Marlin 权重。
278	
279	### 7.5 b12x 环境要求与集成步骤
280	
281	```
282	nvidia-cutlass-dsl                 == 4.5.0.dev0
283	nvidia-cutlass-dsl-libs-base       == 4.5.0.dev0
284	nvidia-cutlass-dsl-libs-cu13       == 4.5.0.dev0   (关键！uv pip 单升 base 会漏)
285	flashinfer-python                  >= 0.6.8.post1
286	torch                              == 2.11.0+cu130
287	CUDA toolkit                       == 13.2
288	export CUTE_DSL_ARCH=sm_120a       (不带 'a' 会 ptxas 拒收 block-scaled MMA)
289	```
290	
291	**b12x 踩过的坑**（记录以防重犯）：
292	
293	1. **cutlass-dsl 4.4.2 → 4.5.0.dev0 的 NVVM lowering**：4.4.2 生成 `_mma.block_scale...` 带下划线前缀（占位符），ptxas 报 `Unexpected instruction types`。4.5.0.dev0 才发 `mma.sync.aligned...kind::mxf4nvf4.block_scale`。必须三包同步升。
294	2. **CUTE_DSL_ARCH 默认不是 sm_120a**：默认回退 `sm_120`（无 arch suffix），block-scaled MMA 需要 `sm_120a`。
295	3. **PR demo 函数 `dense_gemm()` M=1 触发 `cudaErrorIllegalInstruction`**：不用 demo，直接走生产路径 `_compile_block_scaled_gemm` + `gemm.wrapper`（参考 `flashinfer/gemm/gemm_base.py` `_b12x_gemm_fp4_runner`）。
296	4. **Monkey-patch flashinfer.cute_dsl.utils**：我们没升 flashinfer 本体，只从 PR 拉 kernel 文件，运行时注入 `sm120_make_smem_layout_sfa/sfb`。
297	
298	**集成路径**：
299	
300	1. **kernel 文件**：从 `bench/b12x/` 复制到 `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/b12x/`（`dense_blockscaled_gemm_sm120.py` + `cute_dsl_utils.py` + `__init__.py`）
301	2. **glue 模块**：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/b12x_fp4.py`——懒加载、monkey-patch flashinfer.cute_dsl.utils、编译+缓存、`b12x_gemm_fp4(x, w, x_sf, w_sf, alpha, tile, prefetch)` API
302	3. **modelopt_quant.py dispatch**：`NvFp4LinearMethod.apply()` 加第三路
303	4. **prepare_env.sh**：`CUTE_DSL_ARCH=sm_120a`（必须带 `a`）+ 3 包 cutlass-dsl 4.5.0.dev0（环境已具备，验证 BOS 清单）+ `CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/cute_dsl_cache`
304	5. **warmup**：`--skip-server-warmup` 前按 bench 最优 tile 表预编译 5 shape × 6 M_bucket = 30 个 kernel 变体（首次 ~5–10 分钟，后续复用 `CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR`）
305	6. **autotune**：不需要再跑 flashinfer autotune（同 shape 区间已被 b12x 接管）；现有 `mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json` 保留用于 M > 256 的 CUTLASS 路径
306	
307	## 8. 已终结方向（不值得做）
308	
309	| 方向 | 原因 |
310	|---|---|
311	| 手写 pure NVFP4 GEMM kernel | CUTLASS 已用足 TMA + WS + Cooperative + persistent + sm_120 原生 MMA |
312	| Cluster > 1 | sm_120 无 multicast |
313	| 大 K tile (>128) 搭大 M/N tile | smem 不够 2 stage |
314	| 小 tile (<128) | TMA atom 约束 |
315	| flashinfer `trtllm` backend | sm_120 不支持（`cute-dsl` 从 PR #3051 拉 b12x kernel 后可用，见 §7.4） |
316	| sm_120-native W4A16 重写 Marlin | Marlin decode M=1/8 已达 82-97% L2 BW（gate/up/down），无可挤空间 |
317	| Marlin bandwidth 测量作为 blocker | 已测，实证 L2 饱和，终结 |
318

1114	    """Linear method for NVFP4.
1115	    Supports loading NVFP4 checkpoints with the following structure:
1116

1118	    |----------------------------------------------------|

1120	    |weight                | NVFP4(SE2M1)  | [1, X, y/2] |
1121	    |weight_scale          | FP8-E4M3      | [X, Y]      |
1122	    |weight_scale_2        | torch.float32 | scalar      |
1123	
1124	    The weights are quantized per block of 16 elements.
1125	    Args: quant_config: The ModelOpt quantization config.

1129	        self.quant_config = quant_config

1146	            )
1147	
1148	        output_size_per_partition = sum(output_partition_sizes)

1150	
1151	        layer.logical_widths = output_partition_sizes
1152	
1153	        layer.input_size_per_partition = input_size_per_partition
1154	        layer.output_size_per_partition = output_size_per_partition
1155	        if input_size_per_partition % 16 != 0:
1156	            raise ValueError(

1158	            )
1159	
1160	        weight_dtype = (

1163	            else params_dtype

1165	
1166	        weight = ModelWeightParameter(

1173	            input_dim=1,

1175	            weight_loader=weight_loader,

1178

1188	            weight_loader=weight_loader,

1196	                dtype=weight_dtype,
1197	            ),
1198	            input_dim=1,
1199	            output_dim=0,
1200	            weight_loader=weight_loader,
1201	        )
1202	
1203	        layer.register_parameter("weight_scale", weight_scale)
1204

1215	    def process_weights_after_loading(self, layer: torch.nn.Module) -> None:
1216	        input_scale_2 = layer.input_scale.max().to(torch.float32)
1217	        weight_scale_2 = layer.weight_scale_2.max().to(torch.float32)
1218	        layer.input_scale = Parameter(input_scale_2, requires_grad=False)
1219	        layer.weight_scale_2 = Parameter(weight_scale_2, requires_grad=False)
1220	
1221	        # Finalize AWQ pre_quant_scale: move to CUDA and cast to bf16 so that
1222	        # x * pre_quant_scale stays in bf16 without an extra .to() in apply().
1223	        if hasattr(layer, "pre_quant_scale"):
1224	            layer.pre_quant_scale = Parameter(
1225	                layer.pre_quant_scale.data.to(
1226	                    device="cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16
1227	                ),
1228	                requires_grad=False,
1229	            )
1230	        layer.alpha = Parameter(
1231	            layer.input_scale * layer.weight_scale_2, requires_grad=False
1232	        )
1233	        layer.input_scale_inv = Parameter(
1234	            (1 / input_scale_2).to(torch.float32), requires_grad=False
1235	        )
1236	
1237	        # Full Marlin FP4 fallback for non-Blackwell GPUs (SM < 100 but SM >= 75)
1238	        from sglang.srt.layers.quantization.marlin_utils_fp4 import (
1239	            is_fp4_marlin_supported,
1240	            prepare_fp4_layer_for_marlin,
1241	            should_use_fp4_marlin_fallback,
1242	        )
1243	        if should_use_fp4_marlin_fallback() and _MARLIN_HYBRID_THRESHOLD <= 0:
1244	            prepare_fp4_layer_for_marlin(
1245	                layer,
1246	                weight_attr="weight",
1247	                weight_scale_attr="weight_scale",
1248	                weight_global_scale_attr="weight_scale_2",
1249	            )
1250	            layer._use_fp4_marlin = True
1251	            return
1252	
1253	        if FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND == "trtllm":
1254	            # FlashInfer TRTLLM FP4 GEMM requires a different weight layout.
1255	            # FlashInfer provides nvfp4_quantize to quantize + shuffle the
1256	            # layout but we use our own quantization so we have to call
1257	            # shuffles ourselves.
1258	            from flashinfer import shuffle_matrix_a, shuffle_matrix_sf_a
1259	
1260	            weight = layer.weight
1261	            scale = layer.weight_scale
1262	            epilogue_tile_m = 128
1263	            weight = shuffle_matrix_a(weight.view(torch.uint8), epilogue_tile_m)
1264	            scale = (
1265	                shuffle_matrix_sf_a(scale.view(torch.uint8), epilogue_tile_m)
1266	                .reshape(scale.shape)
1267	                .view(torch.float8_e4m3fn)
1268	            )
1269	
1270	            layer.weight_scale_interleaved = Parameter(scale, requires_grad=False)
1271	            layer.weight = Parameter(weight, requires_grad=False)
1272	            return
1273	        # Pad and blockwise interleave weight_scale
1274	        scales = layer.weight_scale
1275	        scale_ndim = scales.ndim
1276	        if scale_ndim == 2:
1277	            scales = scales.unsqueeze(0)
1278	        assert scales.ndim == 3
1279	        B, M, K = scales.shape
1280	        round_up_multiple = lambda x, m: (x + m - 1) // m * m
1281	        M_padded = round_up_multiple(M, 128)
1282	        K_padded = round_up_multiple(K, 4)
1283	        padded_scales = torch.zeros((B, M_padded, K_padded), dtype=scales.dtype)
1284	        padded_scales[:B, :M, :K] = scales
1285	        batches, rows, cols = padded_scales.shape
1286	        assert rows % 128 == 0
1287	        assert cols % 4 == 0
1288	        padded_scales = padded_scales.reshape(batches, rows // 128, 4, 32, cols // 4, 4)
1289	        padded_scales = padded_scales.permute((0, 1, 4, 3, 2, 5))
1290	        padded_scales = padded_scales.contiguous().cuda()
1291	        padded_scales = (
1292	            padded_scales.reshape(M_padded, K_padded)
1293	            if scale_ndim == 2
1294	            else padded_scales.reshape(B, M_padded, K_padded)
1295	        )
1296	        layer.weight_scale_interleaved = Parameter(padded_scales, requires_grad=False)
1297	
1298	        # Hybrid Marlin: prepare Marlin FP4 weights alongside CUTLASS
1299	        # Original weight/weight_scale are still intact after CUTLASS prep above

1302	            layer._hybrid_marlin_threshold = _MARLIN_HYBRID_THRESHOLD
1303	
1304	        if (

def fp4_gemm(
    input: torch.Tensor,
    weight: torch.Tensor,
    input_sf: torch.Tensor,
    weight_sf: torch.Tensor,
    alpha: torch.Tensor,
    out_dtype: torch.dtype,
    out_features: int,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    backend = FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND if FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND else "cutlass"
    if enable_flashinfer_fp4_gemm:
        return flashinfer_fp4_gemm(
            input, weight, input_sf, weight_sf, alpha, out_dtype, backend=backend
        )
    else:
        return cutlass_fp4_gemm(input, weight, input_sf, weight_sf, alpha, out_dtype)


if is_cuda() and (not is_sm120_supported()) and (fp4_quantize is not None):

    @register_fake_if_exists("sgl_kernel::scaled_fp4_quant")
